## origin_unplugged_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin_unplugged_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$CHALLENGING_ORIGIN$\n与同化他们的机器被切断联系之后，这个新生的文明为了在星系中确立自身地位而进行了顽强的斗争。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:4`

### English

$CHALLENGING_ORIGIN$\nAbruptly severed from the Machine that assimilated them, this nascent civilization has fought fiercely to establish its own presence in the galaxy.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:4`

## START_SCREEN_UNPLUGGED

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTART_SCREEN_UNPLUGGED&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的意识在焚天炽地的烈火和粉身碎骨的剧痛中突然清醒过来。五感仿佛被击穿一般，包罗万象的音景突然成为了默片，取而代之的唯有震耳欲聋的死机铃声；一个重低音反复回响在我们多重感官的地狱图景之中。\n\n我们感受到了杀意：是些毫无血肉的杀人机器，还有一些与我们自己的船惊人地相似的领航船。它们最初的攻击如疾风骤雨，却也很不协调。我们只得进行仓促的军事反击。本能驱使着我们与之对抗，并形成一个基本的秩序来凝聚起我们的集体智慧。\n\n这场战役尚未取得胜利，但由于我们分散的部队目前已在[capital_scope.GetName]立足脚跟，希望很快就能取得胜利。然后我们也许就能弄清楚这到底是怎么回事……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:26`

### English

Awareness struck us in a frenzy of scorching weapons fire and crippling pain. Our senses reeled, as though an all-encompassing soundscape had suddenly turned mute. In its place is the deafening ring of a dead transmission; a bass note in the multi-sensory hellscape we now find ourselves in.\n\nSomething wants to kill us: murderous machines, devoid of organic components and piloting ships which bear a striking resemblance to our own. Their initial attack was devastating and sudden, but it was also poorly coordinated. We scrambled a military response. Our instincts have driven us to fight back, form a rudimentary hierarchy, and gather our collective wits.\n\nThis battle is not yet won, but with our scattered forces now secure on [capital_scope.GetName], we hope it soon will be. Then perhaps we can work out what any of this is about...

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:34`

## unplugged_battlefield_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged_battlefield_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在残忍的灭绝之后，该星球上四处遍布着义体人和机械的尸骸。剩下的健康个体和尚能行动的伤员在其间打扫战场，用尸骸上的零件升级躯体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:34`

### English

Countless cyborgs and machines lie dead across this world following a brutal attempt at extermination. Healthy individuals and walking wounded alike may scavenge their bodies for upgrades.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:45`

## trait_unplugged_cybernetic_positives_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atrait_unplugged_cybernetic_positives_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

全肢体替换和义体化纳米框架确保这些义体人能以残酷的效率发动战争。为此，他们牺牲了一些非军事的身体机能。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:38`

### English

Total limb replacements and bonded nanomesh body armor ensure these cyborgs can wage war with brutal efficiency. As a consequence, several non-military bodily functions have been sacrificed.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:50`

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:52`

## trait_unplugged_cybernetic_positives_2_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atrait_unplugged_cybernetic_positives_2_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些义体人受益于整体化负载实施方案。可更换的强化件取代了与躯体高度整合的装甲，使其可承担少量非军事功能。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:41`

### English

These cyborgs benefit from a holistic approach to embodied loadouts. Rather than fully-integrated body armor, swappable augments allow for a modicum of non-military function.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:53`

### 中文

这些义体人的的感官、腺体和运动神经都进行了精心的增强，保障了他们勇猛的战斗力，同时只略微影响生殖能力。

### English

Elegant augmentations to these cyborgs' senses, glands, and motor nerves ensure they remain fierce and capable warriors while only marginally impacting their reproductive capabilities.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:56`

## trait_unplugged_cybernetic_negatives_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atrait_unplugged_cybernetic_negatives_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

部分义体人的增强装置依赖于已废弃的战场网络，导致他们承受极大压力和不适。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:48`

### English

Several of these cyborgs' augments were designed to utilize a battlefield network which no longer exists, causing intense stress and discomfort.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:61`

## trait_unplugged_cybernetic_negatives_3_tt

### English

Happiness When Not in War or Crisis: §R-10%§!

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:62`

## trait_unplugged_cybernetic_negatives_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atrait_unplugged_cybernetic_negatives_2&lang=en)

### English

Disconnected

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:63`

## trait_unplugged_cybernetic_negatives_2_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atrait_unplugged_cybernetic_negatives_2_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

强加在这些义体人身上的军用强化件带来的不适应性极大地降低了他们的生活质量，还增加了他们在康复期间的身体需求。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:51`

### English

The ill-fitting nature of these cyborgs' imposed, military augments greatly reduces their quality of life and increases their bodies' demands during periods of rehabilitation.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:64`

## trait_unplugged_cybernetic_negatives_1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atrait_unplugged_cybernetic_negatives_1&lang=en)

### 中文

故障

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:52`

### English

Haunted

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:65`

## trait_unplugged_cybernetic_negatives_1_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atrait_unplugged_cybernetic_negatives_1_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

从以往的机器意识中产生的幻听和其他的感官回声仍然困扰着这些义体人，他们承受着严重的抑郁和焦虑。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:53`

### English

Auditory hallucinations and other sensory echoes from their former machine consciousness still haunt these cyborgs, causing them to suffer severe bouts of depression and anxiety.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:66`

## trait_unplugged_decyberized

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atrait_unplugged_decyberized&lang=en)

### 中文

去义体化

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:54`

### English

De-Cyberized

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:68`

## trait_unplugged_decyberized_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atrait_unplugged_decyberized_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些前义体人弃用了一度强加在他们身上的强化件，恢复了他们物种的许多有机遗传属性。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:55`

### English

These ex-cyborgs have disabled or cast off the augments which had once been forced upon them, reclaiming many of their species' organic, inherited attributes.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:69`

## trait_unplugged_neurocalmed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atrait_unplugged_neurocalmed&lang=en)

### 中文

神经镇定

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:56`

### English

Neuro-Calmed

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:71`

## trait_unplugged_neurocalmed_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atrait_unplugged_neurocalmed_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些义体人的神经烯的水平被下调，减少了冲动和攻击性，驱使个体放弃个人利益，转而支持社会规范。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:57`

### English

These cyborgs have their Neuroxene levels downregulated, reducing impulsivity and aggression, driving individuals to de-prioritize personal gain in favor of societal norms.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:72`

## trait_unplugged_neurosavant

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atrait_unplugged_neurosavant&lang=en)

### 中文

神经元专家

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:58`

### English

Neuro-Savant

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:74`

## trait_unplugged_neurosavant_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atrait_unplugged_neurosavant_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些义体人的认知强化指数水平经过上调，突触可塑性、信息保留能力和问题解决能力均得到增强。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:59`

### English

These cyborgs have their Cognivex levels upregulated, enhancing synaptic plasticity, information retention, and problem-solving abilities.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:75`

## trait_unplugged_regenoptimized

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atrait_unplugged_regenoptimized&lang=en)

### 中文

再生优化

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:60`

### English

Regen-Optimized

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:77`

## trait_unplugged_regenoptimized_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atrait_unplugged_regenoptimized_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些义体人的再生醇-X的水平被上调，使生物系统与义体系统协调运作，从而让义体植入体能在分子层面实现自我修复。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:61`

### English

These cyborgs have their Regenol-X levels upregulated, harmonizing biological and synthetic systems, allowing cybernetic implants to self-repair at the molecular level.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:78`

## unplugged.100.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.100.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们刚一在这个宇宙中觉醒，这些暴力的机器似乎就想把我们赶尽杀绝。然而，我们发现自己似乎比它们更擅长使用暴力。\n\n所谓的§Y[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]§!今天损失惨重。情报显示，它们的帝国已经支离破碎、面目全非——但肯定不是因为这场小冲突吧？目前我们还所知甚少，至于说我们的…直觉？直觉告诉我们有些东西不太对劲。\n\n传感器在我们附近没有探测到敌对的船只，但发现这个恒星系的§Y[origin_unplugged_pre_ftl_planet.GetName]§!行星正在发出高水平的辐射。\n\n在邻近的星系也发现了残余舰队，它们似乎暂时还按兵不动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:93`

### English

No sooner had we awoken into this universe when these violent machines tried to remove us from it. However, it seems we are more adept at wielding violence than they were.\n\nThe so-called §Y[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]§! has taken a great loss today. Our intel suggests their empire has fragmented beyond recognition - surely not as a result of this minor skirmish? We know so little at the moment, but our... instincts? They tell us something is off.\n\nSensors detect no more hostile ships in our immediate vicinity, but high levels of radiation are emanating from the §Y[origin_unplugged_pre_ftl_planet.GetName]§! planet in this system.\n\nRemnant fleets have also been detected in neighboring systems, seemingly holding their positions.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:114`

### English

## unplugged.115.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.115.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

……哎：[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural]！我们认输。你们打算讨论一下我们的投降条款吗？这就是停止攻击的原因吗？\n\n拜托了，我们的社会支离破碎。我们知道自己输了，你们到底要我们怎么样，快告诉我们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:101`

### English

... -ay again: [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural]! We concede. Do you wish to discuss the terms of our surrender? Is that why you ceased your attack?\n\nPlease, our society is in ruins. We know we are defeated. Tell us how you want us to proceed.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:124`

## unplugged.115.desc.pacifist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.115.desc.pacifist&lang=en)

### 中文

……哎：[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural]！我们准备好讨论投降条款了。这……这就是你们停止攻击的原因吗？\n\n拜托了，你们到底要我们怎么样，快告诉我们！我们不再抵抗了；我们只想要和平共处。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:102`

### English

... -ay again: [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural]! We are ready to discuss the terms of our surrender. Is that.... Is that why you ceased your attack?\n\nPlease, tell us how you wish us to proceed! We offer no more resistance; we desire only peaceful co-existence.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:125`

## unplugged.115.desc.phobe

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.115.desc.phobe&lang=en)

### 中文

……哎：[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural]！我们认输。你们要我们投降吗？这就是你们停止攻击的原因吗？\n\n拜托了，你们到底要我们怎么样，快告诉我们。我们的社会支离破碎；我们只想自己静静。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:103`

### English

... -ay again: [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural]! We concede. Do you want us to surrender? Is that why you ceased your attack?\n\nPlease, tell us how you want to proceed. Our society is in ruins; we just want to be left alone.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:126`

... '[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural]'?!

## unplugged.116.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.116.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

……[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural]？没错吧……？\n\n（他们是在回应我们吧？如果我们搞错了而且他们并没有翻译工具，那我们文明所剩下的断壁残垣就真的，毫无希望……）\n\n……[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural]？！你们能理解我们吗？请回答！我们不希望两国人民之间发生任何误解。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:106`

### English

... [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural]? Are you..?\n\n(They are definitely receiving us, yes? By the stars, people - if we're wrong and they don't have access to translation tools, the remains of our entire civilization are truly, hopelessly...)\n\n... [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural]?! Do you understand us? Please respond! We do not wish for any misunderstanding between our peoples.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:129`

## unplugged.116.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.116.b&lang=en)

### 中文

你说我们是“[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural]”？！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:107`

### English

You think we are the '[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural]'?!

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:130`

## unplugged.120.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.120.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

谢谢，谢谢。我们对您的仁慈深表敬意，[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural]。我谨代表[injured_country.GetName]前来讨论我们的投降条款。\n\n我们已经失去了很多人；许多城市已无法居住。本着公平交易的原则，我必须强调：我们真的已经没什么能给你们的了，你们远远超过了我们的水平。恳请你们加以考虑。我们的小命听您发落。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:108`

### English

Thank you, thank you. We are humbled by your mercy, [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural]. I am empowered to speak on behalf of the [injured_country.GetName] to discuss the terms of our surrender.\n\nWe have lost a great many people; many of our cities are now uninhabitable. In the spirit of fair bargaining I must confess: we have little to offer that you have not already put beyond our grasp. We implore you to take this into consideration. Our lives are in your [injured_country.species.GetHandNamePlural].

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:131`

## unplugged.120.desc.spiri

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.120.desc.spiri&lang=en)

### 中文

谢天谢地！我们对您的仁慈深表敬意，[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural]。我谨代表[injured_country.GetName]前来讨论我们的投降条款。\n\n我们已经损失惨重；我们对你们五体投地。您有什么吩咐？我们的小命听您发落。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:109`

### English

Praises to the above and the below! We are humbled by your mercy, [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural]. I am empowered to speak on behalf of the [injured_country.GetName] to discuss the terms of our surrender.\n\nOur losses have been many; we prostrate ourselves before you. What are your demands? Our lives are in your [injured_country.species.GetHandNamePlural].

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:132`

## unplugged.120.desc.mil_phobe

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.120.desc.mil_phobe&lang=en)

### 中文

感谢您，[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural]。向您致敬。我谨代表[injured_country.GetName]前来讨论我们的投降条款。\n\n你们对我们的社会造成了严重的打击。我们……感谢您的停火。我们悉听尊便。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:110`

### English

Thank you, [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural]. Most honorable. I am empowered to speak on behalf of the [injured_country.GetName] and discuss the terms of our surrender.\n\nYou dealt our society a vicious blow. We... appreciate your ceasefire. Our lives are now in your [injured_country.species.GetHandNamePlural].

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:133`

## unplugged.120.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.120.a&lang=en)

你们一直叫我们[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural]。为什么？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:111`

### English

You keep calling us [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural]. Why?

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:134`

## unplugged.120.b

你们的命归我们了。

Your lives are forfeit.

## unplugged.120.c.tt

§L通讯终止。§!\n我们可以通过在[injured_homeworld.GetName]上建造§Y观测站§!来重启呼叫频道。\n\n

## unplugged.125.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.125.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

……这是什么恶作剧吗？还是在开玩笑？\n\n几个月前，我们的监视器追踪到了你们舰队的到来。我们试着联系，结果你们就这么入侵了我们！我自己也四次死里逃生……一波又一波的登陆艇，装满了机器人和像你一样的强化个体。然后突然之间，你们的机器就停止了入侵。\n\n我们的文明不如你们先进，但这种情况仍然使我们感到困惑。请告诉我们：你的目的是什么，你还在这里做什么？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:116`

### English

... Is this a trick? Some sort of joke?\n\nOur monitors tracked the arrival of your fleet, months ago. We tried to make contact, and instead you invaded us! I myself barely escaped four separate attacks... Waves of landing craft, packed full of robots and augmented individuals just like you. And then all of a sudden, your machines stopped invading.\n\nOur civilization is not as advanced as yours, but still: this situation is baffling to us. Please, enlighten us: what is your intent, and why are you still here?

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:139`

## unplugged.125.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.125.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们[root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]击败了[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:117`

### English

We, the [root.GetSpeciesNamePlural], have defeated the [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural].

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:140`

## unplugged.125.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.125.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们得休会了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:118`

### English

We must adjourn.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:141`

## unplugged.130.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.130.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

你是说[root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]？\n\n我们的人民并不会对你们感恩戴德，但你要知道，我们很高兴看到你们的内斗结束了。终于能和一个…嗯，一个像人的东西说话了。\n\n然而，我们的局面没有改变。[root.GetSpeciesName]，[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetSpeciesName]……对我们来说没有区别。我们遭遇的第一个外星文明是你们，而你们就想毁灭我们。然后你们又发生了内斗，却依旧是把我们蹂躏了一番。\n\n请允许我坦率地说：你现在跟我们的对话是出于友好的目的吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:119`

### English

[root.GetSpeciesNamePlural], you say?\n\nYou will not receive much gratitude from our people, but know that we are glad to see an end to your infighting. It's also a relief to finally speak to a... Well, to a fellow person.\n\nOur situation is unchanged, however. [root.GetSpeciesName], [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetSpeciesName]... It makes no difference. Yours is the first alien civilization we have met, and you tried to eradicate us. Then you broke that off to fight each other instead, and still that nearly ruined us anyway.\n\nAllow me to be frank: do you speak to us now in friendship?

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:142`

## unplugged.130.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.130.a&lang=en)

### 中文

是的。有什么我们能做的吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:120`

### English

We do. How can we help?

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:143`

## unplugged.130.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.130.b&lang=en)

### 中文

至少现在是的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:121`

### English

For now, yes.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:144`

## unplugged.130.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.130.c&lang=en)

### 中文

我们觉得你们不礼貌得让人厌恶。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:122`

### English

We find your lack of respect distasteful.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:145`

## unplugged.131.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.131.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

好，那就好了。很感谢你们不再把我们放在火线上。\n\n如能提供先进的技术为我们建立通讯，我们将不胜感激。我们的命运互相交织在一起，所以我们非常希望保持这一外交渠道的畅通。\n\n反过来，一旦我们有机会重建家园并悼念死者，我们也将尽我们所能向你们提供援助。如果这是你的习惯做同样的事情：相信你自己也会通过铭记历史来变得清醒。\n\n为我们未来的互相理解举杯，[root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]。为了我们的共同利益，希望这就是[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural]的终结了……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:123`

### English

Good. That's a relief. We are grateful to no longer be in your firing line.\n\nWe would appreciate you putting your superior technology towards setting up communications for us. Your fate and ours are now intertwined, so we greatly desire keeping this diplomatic channel open.\n\nWe, in turn, will extend what little aid we can to you - once we have had chance to rebuild, and to mourn our dead. If it is your custom to do the same: we trust your own memorials will bring you some sense of clarity.\n\nHere's to future understanding between us, [root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]. And for both our sakes, here's hoping that really was the last of the [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural]...

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:146`

## unplugged.131.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.131.a&lang=en)

### 中文

谢谢你们，[injured_country.GetSpeciesNamePlural]。

## unplugged_origin_machine_legacy_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged_origin_machine_legacy_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

曾经同化我们整个物种的机械体自己四分五裂了。在我们身后是其统治和残暴的阴燃痕迹。[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]究竟是什么？我们又在其已经失败的计划中扮演着什么样的角色？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:130`

### English

The Machine which once assimilated our entire species has torn itself apart. Behind us lies its smoldering trail of domination and brutality. What was the [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName], and what role did we play in its doomed plans?

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:155`

## unplugged.2000.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2000.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在我们恒星[capital_scope.star.GetName]周围的宇宙辐射中潜伏着微妙的载波信号。\n\n我们从中提取出一段音频流，特别令人难以忘怀的低沉音调混合着扭曲的反响。每个听到这段音频的[root.GetSpeciesAdj]都会感到舒适和亲切，但没有人知道为什么。我们推测这肯定与[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]有关。\n\n信号源似乎就在§Y[origin_unplugged_maker_planet.solar_system.GetName]§!恒星系。我们的星图也相应更新了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:134`

### English

A subtle carrier signal lies dormant in the cosmic radiation surrounding our star, [capital_scope.star.GetName].\n\nWe have isolated an audio stream that is characterized by haunting, deep tones and distorted feedback. Every [root.GetSpeciesAdj] who has heard the stream finds it soothing and familiar, yet none can explain why. We suspect the [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName] must be involved.\n\nThe signal source appears to be in the §Y[origin_unplugged_maker_planet.solar_system.GetName]§! system. Our star charts have been updated accordingly.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:160`

### English

## MACHINE_LEGACY_MAKERS_UNPLUGGED_CAT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMACHINE_LEGACY_MAKERS_UNPLUGGED_CAT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

对[from.GetName]的轨道扫描后我们的系统瞬间被有益的固件更新包轰炸。我们的船现在运行得比以前平稳得多，但这些数据包的性质表明这个星球曾经属于§H[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:139`

### English

An orbital scan of [from.GetName] instantly bombarded our systems with beneficial firmware updates. Our vessel now runs more smoothly than before, but the very nature of these data packets suggest this world once belonged to the §H[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]§!.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:165`

## MACHINE_LEGACY_MAKERS_CAT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMACHINE_LEGACY_MAKERS_CAT&lang=en)

### 中文

$MACHINE_LEGACY_MAKERS_UNPLUGGED_CAT$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:140`

### English

$MACHINE_LEGACY_MAKERS_UNPLUGGED_CAT$

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:166`

## MACHINE_LEGACY_MAKERS_CAT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMACHINE_LEGACY_MAKERS_CAT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对[from.GetName]的扫描发现该行星上有大量被抛弃的科技产物，而且是近期遗弃于此。整个星球上仍跃动着电脉冲信号，许多遥感探测器因此难以维持稳定的朝向。这片错综复杂的电磁场迷雾中究竟潜藏着什么不为人知的秘密呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:141`

### English

Our initial scans of [from.GetName] suggest this planet is rich in discarded technology, but was abandoned only recently. Electrical impulses still dance across that world while many of our remote probes struggle to keep coherent headings. What mysteries lie within the haze of these intricate electromagnetic fields?

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:167`

## unplugged.2005.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2005.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj]遗产

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:142`

### English

The [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj] Legacy

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:168`

## unplugged.2005.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2005.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一些更密集的电磁场口袋在[fromfrom.GetName]四处闪烁着。这些电磁场是某种击穿了许多工厂和船坞的灾难性能量涌造成的。我们的[controller.GetScientistPlural]成功地中和掉了一处这种场，却只发现受影响的设施已经损坏到无法修复了。\n\n这些工厂遍布全球——我们相信这曾经是§H[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]§!的生产中心——因为它将[root.GetName]视为友方舰船。然而，在向我们的飞船发送固件更新后不久，连接就被切断了，几个地区发电站随之出现了级联故障。\n\n我们可能刚刚经历了自身与机械分离的回声，尽管我们还不知道其方式及原因。随着当地威胁的降低，如今我们有机会去发掘[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]到底在这生产什么了——而且也许还能知道自己到底在其统治体系中究竟是个什么样的角色。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:143`

### English

Several more dense pockets of electromagnetism now scintillate across [fromfrom.GetName]. These 'EM' fields are the result of catastrophic power surges which have torn through several factories and shipyards. Our [controller.GetScientistPlural] successfully nullified one such field only to find the facility is now damaged beyond repair.\n\nThe factories covering this world - which we believe to have been a manufacturing hub for §Hthe [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]§! - identified the [root.GetName] as a friendly vessel. However, moments after beaming a firmware update to our ship the connection was severed, causing a cascade failure in several regional power stations.\n\nWe may have just experienced an echo of our own partition from the Machine, though we do not yet know how or why. For now, with local hazards now diminished, we have an opportunity to discover what the [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName] was manufacturing here - and perhaps, the role we played while assimilated into their domain.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:169`

### English

Commence excavating those ruins.

## unplugged.2006.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2006.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[from.GetName]观察到的电脉冲是一场奇异的机械灭绝事件余波。只有在将传感器调整到足以补偿这种强烈的电磁场之后，我们才观察到这里的受破坏程度。这颗星球似乎在几十年前就被机械定居了，然后它们取代或消灭了一个既存的文明。如今，这两个帝国都没有幸存下来，几乎所有定居点都因蓄意破坏或战争而化为废墟。\n\n尽管该前殖民地精巧复杂，但几乎没有什么挽救的余地。我们预计，这种失控的电磁场会在一个世纪左右的时间内烧毁一切。到那时候，[from.GetName]就会变成一片危机四伏的坟场。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:146`

### English

The electrical impulses we observed on [from.GetName] are the lingering result of a bizarre, machine extinction event. Only after our sensors were tuned to compensate for such intense electromagnetism could we observe the full extent of devastation here. The planet looks to have been settled by machines many decades ago, whereupon they displaced or eradicated an existing civilization. None now survive from either empire, with almost the entire settlement reduced to slag by an act of sabotage or warfare.\n\nDespite the sophistication of this former colony, there is little hope of salvage here. We predict the runaway 'EM' fields will burn out in approximately a century. Until then, [from.GetName] remains a singularly hostile graveyard.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:172`

## machine_legacy_makers_site

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amachine_legacy_makers_site&lang=en)

### 中文

工厂设施

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:147`

### English

Factory Setting

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:174`

## machine_legacy_makers_site_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amachine_legacy_makers_site_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一场灾难性的能量涌最近击穿了这座巨大的生产设施——这也是无数服务于[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]的类似设施中的一座。之后，我们的探险队会努力调查这些设施是如何建设的，并试着抢救可能还存在着的技术资产，希望能了解这座设施在我们自己的近期历史中可能发挥的作用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:148`

### English

A catastrophic power surge recently tore through this vast manufacturing facility - one of several which served the [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]. In its wake, our expedition shall endeavor to discover how these facilities came to be, salvage whatever technological assets may still remain, and hopefully learn what role this place may have played in our own recent past.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:175`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2010.name&lang=en)

### 中文

工厂一层

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:149`

### English

Factory Floor

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:176`

## unplugged.2010.desc.unplugged

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2010.desc.unplugged&lang=en)

### 中文

从一开始，我们就可以知道了[from.planet.GetName]上设施的大规模生产能力。地面上到处都是巨大的防爆门，每一扇门现在都被半融化的电机牢牢关闭。我们只好从通风系统强行进入。\n\n我们在其内部发现了一个洞穴般的工厂车间，碎片和数百个义体生物的遗骸散落其间。它们大多数都是机器人：机械师、升降机和其他辅助设施，其中的一些最近才损毁，现在还在着冒烟。然而，在设施的一侧，藏匿着几个被同样的机器人杀害的义体人同伴的腐烂已久的尸体。这些身材矮小、衣衫褴褛的哺乳动物装有眼传感器和复杂的四肢，以及与我们几乎一模一样的[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj]控制节点。我们认为这次屠杀发生在我们脱离[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]的几个星期之内；大多数无人机都是在最近我们到达[from.planet.GetName]的能量涌中损毁的。\n\n对我们而言这是一个残暴而熟悉的场景，尽管在这个场景中，这些挣脱枷锁的义体人最终不敌机械体。因为我们[owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]是士兵，所以才有更好的装备能在这样的屠杀中幸存下来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:150`

### English

From the outset, we could see the facility on [from.planet.GetName] was capable of production on a titanic scale. The landscape above ground is dominated by colossal blast doors, every one of which is now seized and held shut by half-melted motors. Instead we forced our entry via the ventilation system.\n\nInside, we found a cavernous factory floor beset with debris and the remains of hundreds of cybernetic beings. Most are robots: mechanics, lifters, and other auxiliaries, some of whom perished so recently they are still giving off smoke. However, stashed to one side of the facility are the long-decayed bodies of several fellow cyborgs, slain by those same robots. These diminutive and ragged-looking mammalians had been fitted with ocular sensors and intricately tooled limbs, along with [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj] control nodules almost identical to our own. We have dated this massacre to within a few weeks of our own break from the [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]; most of the drones perished more recently, in the power surge which marked our arrival to [from.planet.GetName].\n\nFor us this is a brutal and familiar scene, albeit one in which the liberated cyborgs were no match for their Machine counterparts. Soldiers that we are, we [owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural] were better equipped to survive such slaughter.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:177`

## unplugged.2015.desc.unplugged

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2015.desc.unplugged&lang=en)

### 中文

工厂设施的底层除了大屠杀以外还发现了一些地下机库，少量储存着各种战车、导弹平台和机甲。许多硬件并不齐全或已经被这场席卷[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj]机器人的能量涌所严重损毁——但还是剩下了很多值得我们研究出一些结论的东西。\n\n例如：所有驾驶舱和界面的设计都不是为了容纳之前发现的小型哺乳类义体人的，而更像是……为我们设计。此外，解剖学和建筑调查表明，哺乳类义体人不仅是这个星球的原生物种，而且它们可能在被同化之前就建造了这些庞大的工厂。\n\n建筑倒塌和几扇防爆门阻碍我们进一步进入该设施，但相信可以清扫出通往研发中心的道路。或者，一系列的通风井也暗示了设施下面的栖息地——也许是这个星球上的原住民居住的地方。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:154`

### English

Away from the carnage of this facility's factory floor lies a series of subterranean hangars, lightly stocked with various tanks, missile platforms and mech suits. Most of this hadware is incomplete, or else was put beyond use by the same power surge which neutralized the [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj] robots - but enough remains for us draw some interesting conclusions.\n\nFor instance: every cockpit and interface has been designed not to accommodate the small mammalian cyborgs we found earlier, but rather... us. Furthermore, autopsies and architectural surveys suggest the mammalian cyborgs were not only native to this world, but they may have built these sprawling factories prior to their assimilation.\n\nFurther progress into the facility is impeded by structural collapse and a few more blast doors, but we believe we may clear a path towards what looks to be a research and development center. Alternatively, a series of ventilation shafts suggests habitats down beneath the facility - perhaps where the planet's natives resided.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:181`

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:183`

## unplugged.2021.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2021.name&lang=en)

### 中文

受迫设计者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:157`

### English

Designers Under Duress

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:184`

## unplugged.2021.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2021.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

发掘这座设施的研发辅翼是一项艰巨的任务。有难以计数的哺乳类义体人的生命在此终结，他们可能在这里被训练成了技师、工程师甚至是义体专家。这些专家设计了陈列在外面的大多数武器，而我们发现了他们甚至还负责许多我们身上强化件的证据。奇怪的是，尽管他们使用的多数装备是来自[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj]，但是我们也找到了一些品质上乘的定做工具，其设计彰显着与原始有机形式更优良的匹配度。\n\n我们开始整理出这个高等科技社会突然被唐突整合入[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]的时间线。推测机械体可能才到这里不超过几十年；许多生产技术都是本土的。幸好这些技艺高超的专家并没有归顺[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]太长时间，否则如果要对付他们，我们就要面对更加强大的武器装备了。相反，我们活了下来，有机会重拾起该物种的研究传奇。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:158`

### English

Excavating the R&D wing of this facility has been a grisly task. Significantly more of the mammalian cyborgs met their end here, where they had been deployed as technicians, engineers, and even cyberneticists. These specialists designed most of the weaponry arrayed outside, and we found evidence they were even responsible for many of our own augments. Intriguingly, whereas most of the equipment they used is [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj] in origin, we also found several bespoke tools of a superior quality, with design flourishes much better suited to their own organic forms.\n\nWe begin to reveal a timeline in which a society of advanced technicians was abruptly assimilated by the [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]. We suspect the Machine had not been here longer than a few decades; many of these manufacturing techniques appear indigenous. It may be fortunate that such gifted technologists did not remain part of the [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName] for long, else when it turned against us, it would have wielded an even more potent arsenal. Instead, we remain alive now to pick through this species' potent legacy of research.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:185`

## unplugged.2022.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2022.name&lang=en)

### 中文

古代[from.planet.GetName]

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:159`

### English

Old [from.planet.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:186`

## unplugged.2022.desc.unplugged

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2022.desc.unplugged&lang=en)

### 中文

在地表设施的下面蜿蜒着一座勉强宜居的巨大隧道网络。义体人的尸体四处都是；所有原住民都被[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj]屠戮殆尽了。有些人是受到直接的攻击，但我们也发现上面的通风系统被动了手脚。窒息似乎才是这里大多数人的死因。\n\n我们可以从尸体解剖和人类学研究中拼凑出一个大致的时间线。有证据表明，当[from.planet.GetName]星球的原住民与机械接触并突然被同化时，这里早就是一个繁荣的太空时代社会。在约几十年的时间里，[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]将他们的洞察力和生产力投入到开发令人印象深刻的战争机器中，结果这种文化的独特性又被剥夺了，和我们的命运如出一辙。\n\n这个星球上的原生义体人身材娇小而脆弱，无法与他们参与制造的致命机器人相抗衡。他们的大部分身份已经从自己的历史记录中抹去了。我们只能猜测他们在遇到[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]之前的生活状态。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:160`

### English

Sprawling below the surface facility is a vast network of barely habitable tunnels. Cyborg bodies are strewn about everywhere down here; none of these natives survived the [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj] purge. Some were attacked physically, but we also found evidence of tampering in the ventilation systems above. Suffocation seems to have accounted for most who died here.\n\nWe are able to piece together a rough timeline from autopsies and anthropological study. There is evidence that this was already a thriving, space age society by the time the natives of [from.planet.GetName] made contact with the Machine, and were abruptly assimilated. Over a span of no more than decades, the [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName] channeled their insights and productivity into developing an impressive war machine, only to have this culture's uniqueness ripped from it again, the same way we were.\n\nThe small, frail bodies of this planet's native cyborgs were no match for the deadly robots they helped build. Much of who they were has since been wiped from their own historical records. We can only guess what their lives were like before these people met the [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:187`

## unplugged.2030.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2030.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

长程扫描发现[origin_unplugged_machine_legacy_core.GetName]恒星系中突然有新的动作！似乎有一支舰队响应我们对[fromfrom.planet.GetName]的发掘而召集了起来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:162`

### English

Long-range sensors have picked up sudden movement in the [origin_unplugged_machine_legacy_core.GetName] system! A hostile fleet appears to have mustered in direct response to our excavations on [fromfrom.planet.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:190`

## unplugged.2036.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2036.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们通过对刚刚摧毁的[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj]舰队的通讯进行回溯发现了一个围绕[origin_unplugged_taken_planet.GetName]的信标。这似乎会是我们旅程的下一步。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:164`

### English

We have back-traced communications from the [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj] fleet we just destroyed to a buoy orbiting [origin_unplugged_taken_planet.GetName]. This would seem to be our next step on our journey.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:192`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2036.a&lang=en)

## unplugged.2037.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2037.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

使用自己的前[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj]硬件，我们追踪到了到前往[origin_unplugged_maker_planet.GetName]的舰队被摧毁前的最后一次通讯。似乎我们接下来要去的地方就在§Y[origin_unplugged_taken_planet.GetName]§!的轨道上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:166`

### English

Using our own ex-[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj] hardware, we have managed to trace a last-ditch communication burst from the destroyed fleet bound for [origin_unplugged_maker_planet.GetName]. It would seem the next step in our journey lies in orbit of §Y[origin_unplugged_taken_planet.GetName]§!.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:194`

## unplugged.2038.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2038.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

使用自己的前[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj]硬件，我们已经追踪到敌方舰队与[origin_unplugged_maker_planet.GetName]行星的杂散通讯信号。似乎在[origin_unplugged_taken_planet.GetName]的轨道上有些我们感兴趣的东西。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:167`

### English

Using our own ex-[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj] hardware, we have managed to trace a stray communication burst from the hostile fleet linked to planet [origin_unplugged_maker_planet.GetName]. It would seem something of interest lies in orbit of [origin_unplugged_taken_planet.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:195`

### 中文

我们成功追踪到一艘最近击败的[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj]飞船发出的最后一股无法破译的静电流。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:170`

### English

We managed to trace a final stream of indecipherable static from a recently defeated [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj] ship to this location.

## MACHINE_LEGACY_TAKEN_UNPLUGGED

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMACHINE_LEGACY_TAKEN_UNPLUGGED&lang=en)

### 中文

具现时代悖论

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:171`

### English

Embodied Anachronisms

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:200`

## MACHINE_LEGACY_TAKEN_UNPLUGGED_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMACHINE_LEGACY_TAKEN_UNPLUGGED_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[from.GetName]上侦测到了一处环境异常。它的大气带有我们认为与中世纪金属加工有关的各种污染的痕迹，然而我们的表面扫描却被一种理应在在机械星球上出现的奇异辐射所掩盖。\n\n在重新校准传感器之前，我们不能指望收集到更多的数据。这是时间异常，还是其他物种被[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]同化的残余物？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:172`

### English

We have detected an environmental anomaly on [from.GetName]. Its atmosphere bears traces of the kinds of pollution we would associate with medieval metalworking, yet our surface scans are obscured by exotic radiation of a kind we would expect to see on a Machine World.\n\nWe cannot hope to gather more data before first recalibrating our sensors. Is this a temporal anomaly, or else the blighted remnants of another species assimilated by the [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]?

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:201`

## unplugged.2100.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2100.name&lang=en)

### 中文

进程中断

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:173`

### English

Disrupted Progress

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:202`

## unplugged.2100.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2100.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

不管是什么原因导致[fromfrom.GetName]变成现在这种不适宜居住的状态，至少我们关于时间旅行的疯狂理论是可以排除的。事实证明，重新校准传感器并没有我们担心的那么费力。\n\n在这些大气辐射之下有明确的证据表明[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]曾经来过这里。它似乎要么同化吸收了一个前超光速文明，要么就是彻底消灭了他们。我们已经确定了至少一个具有明显文化意义的加固的遗址，议在那里建立一个挖掘站点。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:174`

### English

Whatever caused planet [fromfrom.GetName] to reach its current uninhabitable state, we can at least rule out our wilder theories about time travel. Recalibrating our sensors proved to be a less taxing job than we had feared.\n\nBeneath all this atmospheric radiation is clear evidence that the [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName] maintained a presence here. It appears to have either assimilated and removed a pre-FTL civilization, or else exterminated them outright. We have identified at least one fortified site of apparent cultural significance, where we propose to establish a dig site.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:203`

## unplugged.2100.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2100.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们必须勤奋挖掘。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:175`

### English

We must dig with diligence.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:204`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMACHINE_LEGACY_TAKEN_CAT&lang=en)

### 中文

$MACHINE_LEGACY_TAKEN_UNPLUGGED$

### English

$MACHINE_LEGACY_TAKEN_UNPLUGGED$

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:205`

## MACHINE_LEGACY_TAKEN_CAT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMACHINE_LEGACY_TAKEN_CAT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

对[root.GetName]初步扫描的结果令人费解。它的大气中包含着痕量来自原始冶金技术的污染物，然而表面扫描却发现了复杂的太空级合金。航拍图像上可以看到[from.owner.GetPreFTLLower]社会的聚落，然而堡垒建筑上却装有面对空激光武器。我们建议对这一不一致性进行进一步研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:177`

### English

Results from our initial scans of [root.GetName] do not make proper sense. Its atmosphere bears traces of pollution from primitive metalworking, yet our surface scans found evidence of sophisticated, space-worthy alloys. Aerial imagery shows settlements from a [from.owner.GetPreFTLLower] society, yet their fortified buildings are fitted with surface-to-air laser weaponry. We recommend studying these inconsistencies further.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:206`

## unplugged.2105.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2105.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$unplugged.2100.name$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:178`

### English

$unplugged.2100.name$

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:207`

## unplugged.2105.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2105.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们越是研究这颗看起来是[owner.GetPreFTLLower][planet.GetClassAdj]星球的[planet.GetName]，就发现越多矛盾点，直到开始对一处古战场进行地下调查。尽管经历了数百年的岁月侵蚀，我们还是发现了一些外星人造访的证据：这个星球居住着一个聪慧的[owner.GetPreFTLLower]社会并遭到了义体整合，然而他们的社会结构却被不明原因保留了下来。从对该社会的好意但拙劣的提升，到恶劣的臣服方式实践，目前的理论猜想层出不穷。\n\n这些[owner.GetPreFTLLowerPlural]是单纯被遗忘了还是说我们找到了一个被抛下的社会学实验遗产？我们可能不会有答案了，但在一段时间内，这个遗址肯定会带来相当大的科学价值。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:179`

### English

The more we studied the apparently [owner.GetPreFTLLower] [planet.GetClassAdj] world of [planet.GetName] the more inconsistencies we found, until we began subterranean surveys of an old battlefield. Depsite centuries of decay, we found evidence of alien visitation to this world: home to a genuinely [owner.GetPreFTLLower] society which was cybernetically assimilated, but whose society was then left alone for reasons we can only speculate upon. Current theories range from a well-intended but botched attempt to uplift this society, through to a brutal practice of subjugation techniques.\n\nWere these [owner.GetPreFTLLowerPlural] simply forgotten about, or have we uncovered the remains of a discarded sociological experiment? We may never know, but the site is sure to yield considerable scientific merit for some time yet.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:208`

## machine_legacy_taken_site

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amachine_legacy_taken_site&lang=en)

### 中文

铜与铬

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:180`

### English

Copper and Chrome

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:210`

## machine_legacy_taken_site_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amachine_legacy_taken_site_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在一个曾经青翠的峡谷中存在着一个考古学上的悖论：一个用手工工具建造的坚固定居点，除了简陋的锻炉之外没有任何先进的东西，但却居住着现已死亡的义体人。这些中世纪[owner.GetPreFTLLowerPlural]是谁？[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]又为什么觉得他们有同化价值？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:181`

### English

Nestled within a once-verdant canyon is an archaeological paradox: a fortified settlement which was built using hand tools and hosted nothing more advanced than a crude forge, yet was inhabited by now-dead cyborgs. Who were these medieval [owner.GetPreFTLLowerPlural], and why did the [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName] see fit to assimilate them?

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:211`

## unplugged.2110.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2110.name&lang=en)

### 中文

长矛与脉冲激光

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:182`

### English

Pikes and Pulse Lasers

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:212`

## unplugged.2110.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2110.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

通过初步扫描在离堡垒很近的地方发现了一处战场，让我们可以很容易获得类似的当地的[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj]文物。基于对残余物的断层扫描，我们估计有数千位被[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]同化的[from.planet.GetName]当地居民最终战死于此。但他们也不是此处唯一一种义体人。\n\n在几个世纪前，我们屠戮了这些[owner.GetPreFTLLowerPlural]。我们的团队发现了[owner.GetSpeciesAdj][owner.species.GetRemnantNamePlural]以及一些我们物种强化件的早期型号。战场战略评估显示我们的人是作为突击部队部署在这里的。战斗快速而血腥；这些[owner.GetPreFTLLowerPlural]缺乏协调损失惨重，尽管他们的湿件更加复杂。\n\n我们注意到附近有一座医疗设施的遗迹；看来我们的前辈是从这家医院攻破堡垒的。也许在那里，我们可以了解到很久以前，义体人在这里发生战斗的方式和原因。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:183`

### English

Preliminary surveys found a battlefield very near to the fort, granting us easy access to indigenous and [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj] artifacts alike. Based on a cross-sectional study of the remains, we estimate several thousand native inhabitants of [from.planet.GetName] had been assimilated by the [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName], only to fight and die on this site. They were not the only cyborgs present at this conflict, however.\n\nA mere few centuries ago, we slaughtered these [owner.GetPreFTLLowerPlural]. Our team has identified [owner.GetSpeciesAdj] [owner.species.GetRemnantNamePlural] along with early prototypes of our species' augments. A strategic assessment of the battlefield suggests our people were deployed here as shock troops. The battle was swift and bloody; the [owner.GetPreFTLLowerPlural] lacked co-ordination and suffered heavy losses, despite wielding more sophisticated wetware.\n\nWe note the ruins of a medical facility nearby; it would seem our predecessors breached the fort via this hospital. Perhaps there we may learn how and why cyborg was pitted against cyborg here, so long ago.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:213`

粗暴。

### English

Brutal.

## unplugged.2115.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2115.name&lang=en)

### 中文

晶体管和止血带

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:185`

### English

Transistors and Tourniquets

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:215`

## unplugged.2115.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2115.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

战斗像脉冲炮一样席卷了整座医院。里面找得到的尸体全都不是[owner.GetSpeciesAdj]；只有义体化的[from.planet.GetName]原住民尸横满地。其中大部分都是医院里的病患，然而其中只有极少部分身上真的有战斗创伤。相反，这些病房中有好几个是在通过非常原始的方式进行义体增强件的移除手术。\n\n这座医院很可能并不是遭受到连带损伤，而就是[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]进攻的首要目标。在仔细研究了里面的医学数据之后，我们逐渐了解了个中缘由。[from.planet.GetName]的居民拥有许多独特的演化特质——尤其是一种特别顽强的免疫系统——机械体对此觊觎已久\n\n然而，这些优势也给同化带来了挑战。像我们这样直接移植现有的强化件是不可行的；必须进行适应性调整。这使得该物种变成了更强大的士兵，但也只持续了很短的时间。这些[owner.GetPreFTLLowerPlural]似乎维持了某种自主意识的迹象，坚持旧有的生活方式，因此我们才被派来消灭他们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:186`

### English

Conflict tore through this hospital like a pulse cannon. None of the remains we found inside are [owner.GetSpeciesAdj]; only the cyberized natives of [from.planet.GetName] died here. Most were patients at this facility, however very few of them seemed to sustain actual battle wounds. Instead several of these wards handled removal of cybernetic augments, albeit by very primitive means.\n\nRather being a collateral target, the hospital may well have been the primary objective for the attacking [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]. After scrutinizing the hard-copy medical data within, we begin to see why. The inhabitants of [from.planet.GetName] possessed several uniquely evolved characteristics - not least a singularly resilient immune system - which the Machine will have coveted for its cyborg troops.\n\nHowever, these advantages also presented challenges for assimilation. It could not transplant existing augments from species like ours; it had to adapt. Doing so turned this species into even more capable soldiers, but only for a short time. These [owner.GetPreFTLLowerPlural] appear to have retained some semblance of autonomy, clinging to their old habits and way of life, and so we were deployed to destroy them.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:216`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2115.a&lang=en)

### 中文

看来我们的任务完成得很好……

### English

Clearly we handled that assignment well...

## unplugged.2121.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2121.name&lang=en)

### 中文

适应性同化

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:189`

### English

Adaptive Assimilation

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:219`

## unplugged.2121.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2121.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在一座废弃的附属医院里埋藏着大量用来强化和维护这些[root.GetPreFTLLowerPlural]义体人的零件。这与我们此前发现的杂乱无章的病房设备不同，这些设备与太空时代大的义体人是同一科技水平；显然来自[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj]。其中一些工具似乎已经联网，使用原始的人工智能来适应即时手术，然而，即使这样的适应性措施也不足以维持对[from.planet.GetName]人口的同化。\n\n尽管这个已有几百年历史的机器被忽视了，但它仍然可以为我们洞悉自我维持的原理——甚至可以帮我们更深入理解同化的原理。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:190`

### English

Tucked away in a derelict hospital annex was a wealth of bespoke apparatus for augmenting and maintaining these [root.GetPreFTLLowerPlural] cyborgs. Unlike the hodgepodge ward equipment we've uncovered so far, these devices are contemporary with space age cybernetics; clearly [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj] in origin. Some of these tools appear to have been networked, using primitive AI to adapt surgeries on-the-fly, however even these adaptations were insufficient to keep the population of [from.planet.GetName] assimilated.\n\nDespite the neglect imposed upon this now centuries-old machinery, it may still provide us with vital insights into our own self-maintenance - or indeed help us better understand the principles of assimilation.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:220`

## unplugged.2125.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2125.name&lang=en)

### 中文

尝试，错误，埋葬

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:191`

### English

Trial, Error and Entombment

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:221`

## unplugged.2125.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2125.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们了解到，[from.planet.GetName]的原住民会在埋葬前保留遗骸的一部分——即使在被同化后他们也坚持这种做法。跟着医院记录，我们来到附近一座杂草丛生的陵墓，这里似乎没有受到冲突的影响。这一重大发现为我们带来了迄今为止最清晰的关于[from.planet.GetName]居民生存方式的图景。他们不仅抵制强化，甚至还能“去义体化”。\n\n我们在完整的义体人旁边也发现了原始的有机遗骸，甚至有几个人在自然死亡前几个月还留下了手术疤痕组织。更多的人似乎是在手术过程中死亡的。即便拥有最高级的免疫系统，他们中世纪水平的去义体化手术成功率也相当之低。尽管如此，无论是成功的还是那些失败的，我们还是可以从这里进行的手术试验中学到很多经验和教训。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:192`

### English

We have learned that the natives of [from.planet.GetName] chose to partially preserve their dead prior to burial - a practice they held to even after assimilation. Hospital records led us to a nearby overgrown mausoleum, untouched by conflict. This bounty of a find gives us our clearest picture yet of how the inhabitants of [from.planet.GetName] not only resisted augmentation, but were able to 'de-cyberize'.\n\nWe found original, organic remains alongside full cyborgs, and even a few individuals with surgical scar tissue formed months before deaths by natural causes. Many more appear to have died while being operated on. Despite having superlative immune systems, their medieval approach to de-cyberization clearly had a rather low success rate. Still, there is much we might learn from the surgical trials conducted here - successful and otherwise.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:222`

## unplugged.2125.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2125.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们要吸取教训。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:193`

### English

Let us learn from their examples.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:223`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2125.a.tt&lang=en)

我们要在自身去义体化工作上调配更多资源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:194`

### English

We may devote additional resources to 'de-cyberizing' ourselves.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:224`

## unplugged.2130.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2130.name&lang=en)

### 中文

埋葬的信标

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:195`

### English

Buried Beacon

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:225`

## unplugged.2130.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2130.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在堡垒下面发现了一座隐藏的地库。里面有一个看起来是某种机械基质的东西：一个退化的节点，能够传播整个机械智能的半独立化身。我们认为这是为了直接控制顽固抵抗的当地人所做的最后努力。这种技术不同于我们之前在[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]那里遇到的任何技术；进一步的研究肯定会推动我们自己的人工智能研究。\n\n在拆除其现已失效的通信阵列的过程中，我们注意到这个化身有一个完整的专属接收回路，可以监控来自[creators_planet.solar_system.GetName]星系的信号。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:196`

### English

A hidden vault has been revealed beneath the fortress proper. Inside is what looks to have been a Machine substrate: a devolved node, capable of propagating a nearly-independent avatar of the entire Machine Intelligence. We assume this was a last-ditch effort to assert direct control of the stubbornly resistant locals. The technology is unlike anything we've encountered before from the [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]; further study is sure to advance our own AI research.\n\nIn the act of tearing down its now-defunct communications array, we noted that this avatar had dedicated an entire receiver circuit to monitoring signals from the [creators_planet.solar_system.GetName] system.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:226`

## unplugged_machine_legacy_creators_poi

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged_machine_legacy_creators_poi&lang=en)

### 中文

接收回路

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:197`

### English

Receiver Circuit

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:227`

## unplugged_machine_legacy_creators_poi_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged_machine_legacy_creators_poi_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一整座已失效的[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj]通讯阵列曾被用于侦测来自此地的信号。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:198`

### English

An entire element of a now-defunct [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj] communications array was dedicated to monitoring signals from this location.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:228`

## MACHINE_LEGACY_CREATORS_UNPLUGGED

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMACHINE_LEGACY_CREATORS_UNPLUGGED&lang=en)

### 中文

蒙尘监视器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:200`

### English

Dust Monitor

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:231`

## MACHINE_LEGACY_CREATORS_UNPLUGGED_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMACHINE_LEGACY_CREATORS_UNPLUGGED_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

在一个别无值得一提的星系中，有一座复杂的监测站孤零零地矗立着，它早已荒废到难以修复的程度。它在监测什么，它又为什么会聚焦在[fromfrom.GetName]中这样一个狭小的区域呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:201`

### English

Alone in a system devoid of other noteworthy phenomena is a sophisticated monitoring station, long since decommissioned and put beyond repair. What was it monitoring, and why was it focused on such a tightly enclosed region of [fromfrom.GetName]?

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:232`

## MACHINE_LEGACY_CREATORS_CAT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMACHINE_LEGACY_CREATORS_CAT&lang=en)

### 中文

蒙尘监视器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:202`

### English

Dust Monitor

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:233`

## MACHINE_LEGACY_CREATORS_CAT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMACHINE_LEGACY_CREATORS_CAT_DESC&lang=en)

在一个别无值得一提的星系中，有一座复杂的监测站孤零零地矗立着，它早已荒废到难以修复的程度。它在监测什么，它又为什么会聚焦在[fromfrom.GetName]中这样一个狭小的区域呢？

## unplugged.2200.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2200.name&lang=en)

### 中文

溜号的扫描站

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:204`

### English

Absence Scanner

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:235`

## unplugged.2200.desc.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2200.desc.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[fromfrom.GetName]的后续研究结果令人沮丧。这座监测站很有可能已经偏离了最初的指向，我们在它当前的轨道上找不到任何有趣的东西。我们基于轨道影响——也就是设备上的凹痕——模拟了其可能的运动，随后相信这座站点最初可能是位于遥远北方的一个陨石坑高原上空。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:205`

### English

Our follow-on studies at [fromfrom.GetName] have proven disappointing. It is possible the monitoring station has been knocked off its original alignment, for we can find nothing of interest beneath its current orbit. We simulated possible movements based on orbital impacts - dents upon the device's hull - and subsequently believe this station may originally have been poised over a cratered plateau far to the north.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:236`

## unplugged.2200.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2200.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$unplugged.2200.desc.intro$\n\n至于设备本身：我们发现这座监测站和[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj]的硬件具有相似之处。$unplugged.2200.desc.outro$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:206`

### English

$unplugged.2200.desc.intro$\n\nAs for the device itself: we note this monitoring station bears a passing resemblance to [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj] hardware. $unplugged.2200.desc.outro$

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:237`

## unplugged.2200.desc.scrapped

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2200.desc.scrapped&lang=en)

### 中文

$unplugged.2200.desc.intro$\n\n至于这座监测站本身：尽管打捞者进行了无情的洗劫，但整体结构仍能看出与[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj]的硬件有几分相似。$unplugged.2200.desc.outro$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:207`

### English

$unplugged.2200.desc.intro$\n\nAs for the monitoring station itself: despite ruthless ransacking by salvagers, the overall structure still bears a passing resemblance to [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj] hardware. $unplugged.2200.desc.outro$

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:238`

## unplugged.2200.desc.others

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2200.desc.others&lang=en)

### 中文

$unplugged.2200.desc.intro$\n\n至于监测站本身：我们还未遇见过类似的设计。尽管空间站上的硬件设备已经过时，但其上层建筑的材料构成异常纯净。我们不知道这座空间站是否有任何情感价值，但肯定有拆解的价值。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:208`

### English

$unplugged.2200.desc.intro$\n\nAs for the monitoring station itself: we have not encountered designs like this before. Although the hardware on board is as outdated as it is broken, the station superstructure is formed of unusually pure materials. We cannot know if the station has any sentimental worth, but there is definitely scrap value to be had here.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:239`

## unplugged.2200.desc.outro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2200.desc.outro&lang=en)

### 中文

它的设计更加粗糙，以至于如果是不太熟悉的人可能会认为它来自一个完全不同的文化。然而，我们相信这甚至会是机械体最早的航天卫星之一。\n\n到目前为止，这颗[fromfrom.GetClassAdj]星球还没有别的什么值得一提的，但是如果我们想尝试了解[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]为什么对这里感兴趣，已经有一支考古调查小组整装待发了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:209`

### English

Its design is more crude, to the extent a less familiar observer might think it from an entirely different culture. However, we believe this may even be one of the Machine's earliest spacefaring satellites.\n\nSo far little else bears mentioning about this [fromfrom.GetClassAdj] world, but an archaeological survey team stands ready should we wish to try and learn what interests the [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName] had here.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:240`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2200.a&lang=en)

只要还有机会……就继续。

If there is a chance... Proceed.

We will find nothing here. Let us move on.

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2200.c&lang=en)

把它拆成零件。

### English

Scrap it for parts.
