## machine_legacy_creators_site

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amachine_legacy_creators_site&lang=en)

### 中文

尘土之下

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:213`

### English

Dust Upon Dust

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:245`

## machine_legacy_creators_site_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amachine_legacy_creators_site_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[from.planet.GetName]上的漫天黄沙中，曾有某种[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj]监测站感兴趣的东西。其硬件现在已经过于陈旧无法修复，但它甚至比我们发现的几乎所有其他机械制品都要早。可能在我们被同化之前它就开始运作了。虽然现在这里很有可能除了沙漠一无所有，但只有一个办法可以完全确定……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:214`

### English

Somewhere in the sand-swept landscape of [from.planet.GetName] once lay something of interest to a [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj] monitoring station. The hardware is beyond repair now, and so old, it predates nearly every other machine artifact we've found. It may even have been in operation since before we were assimilated. There is a chance nothing now remains here except desert, but there's one way we might be truly sure...

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:246`

## unplugged.2210.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2210.name&lang=en)

### 中文

大尘暴区

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:215`

### English

Great Dustbowl

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:247`

## unplugged.2210.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2210.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

最终，我们在这漫漫黄沙之下找到了定居点的证据：一种广泛存在的浅痕，似乎是早期太空时代文明的痕迹。他们的建筑已经摧毁殆尽；废墟中即使是最坚固的部分也被夷为平地。然而，化学分析表明，[from.planet.GetName]的沙漠化是在星球灭绝之后的一段时间才发生的。到目前为止，我们只能在大量建筑材料的原子化残留物中继续挖掘。\n\n我们沿着这座旧城的道路勾勒出的许多模糊轮廓，它们都被一个大陨石坑的边缘所分割——这是一个明显的撞击地点的遗迹。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:216`

### English

Eventually we found evidence of dwellings here under the sand: widespread but shallow traces of what looks to have been an early space age civilization. Their buildings were obliterated; even the most substantial among these ruins have been razed down to their foundations. However, chemical analysis shows the desertification of [from.planet.GetName] came some time after this planet-wide annihilation. Much of what we have been forced to dig through so far is the atomized remains of construction material.\n\nMany of the faint outlines we have traced along thoroughfares in this former city are bisected by the lip of a large crater - remnant of an apparent impact site.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:248`

## unplugged.2215.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2215.name&lang=en)

### 中文

坑洞底部

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:217`

### English

Crater Floor

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:249`

## unplugged.2215.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2215.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

由于集中在撞击坑内的材料数量庞大，我们不得不依赖微调的扫描仪，而不是实际的挖掘进行研究。很快我们就排除了陨石撞击的可能性。这个地点没有任何外星物质的痕迹；相反，这个坑洞是由一次大爆炸造成的——几乎可以肯定这是一种终结文明的武器。\n\n从研究背景看，罪魁祸首应该就是[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]，但这是我们第一次看到机械体如此彻底地从历史上抹去一个文明的证据。我们找不到任何其他线索来说明这些人是谁，以及他们是如何生活的。[from.planet.GetName]就这么消失得无影无踪了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:218`

### English

Due to the sheer amount of material collected inside this crater, we had to rely on finely-tuned scanners more than actual excavation. Very soon we were able to rule out meteor impact. There is no trace of extraterrestrial material at this site; instead this depression was carved out by a massive explosion - almost certainly a civilization-ending weapon.\n\nContext would suggest the [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName] were responsible, but this would be the first we've seen evidence of the Machine wiping a civilization so thoroughly from the historical record. We can find nothing else to suggest who these people were, nor how they lived. [from.planet.GetName] has been swept clean.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:250`

## unplugged.2205.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2205.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj]地库信标

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:219`

### English

[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj] Vault Beacon

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:252`

## unplugged.2205.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2205.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在附近的星系中检测到一个标称来自[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj]数据库的信标信号。它的传输非常微弱，但指向了我们舰船最近访问了的[origin_unplugged_creators_planet.GetName]上空的监测站。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:220`

### English

A beacon signal which claims to originate from an [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj] data vault has been detected in a nearby system. The transmission is exceedingly weak, but it was directed towards our ship that recently visited the monitoring station above [origin_unplugged_creators_planet.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:253`

## unplugged.2205.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2205.a&lang=en)

### 中文

值得调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:221`

### English

Worthy of investigation.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:254`

## unplugged_machine_legacy_unplugged_poi

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged_machine_legacy_unplugged_poi&lang=en)

### 中文

地下数据库信号

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:222`

### English

Data Vault Signal

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:255`

## unplugged_machine_legacy_unplugged_poi_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged_machine_legacy_unplugged_poi_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一座可能属于[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj]的地下数据库在我们遭遇到那座失能的监控站之后开始向我们广播自身的坐标。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:223`

### English

A supposed [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj] data vault has broadcast its position to us after we encountered a defunct monitoring station.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:256`

## unplugged.2300.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2300.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$unplugged.2205.name$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:225`

### English

$unplugged.2205.name$

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:259`

## unplugged.2300.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2300.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

将我们呼唤到此的信标现在的广播向量又清晰，详细引导我们到了星球表面上的一处交互界面前。标准的安保预防措施已经就位；一支队伍准备通过遥控上行链路开始查询这个所谓的“数据地库”。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:226`

### English

The beacon which summoned us here is now broadcasting loud and clear, directing us more specifically to an interface on the planet surface. Standard safety precautions are already in place; a team stands ready to interrogate this so-called 'data vault' by means of remote uplink.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:260`

## unplugged.2300.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2300.a&lang=en)

### 中文

感觉有种奇怪的熟悉感。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:227`

### English

Feels strangely familiar.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:261`

## unplugged.2300.a.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2300.a.tt&lang=en)

### 中文

[from.GetName]恒星系出现一个特殊项目。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:228`

### English

A special project appears in the [from.GetName] system.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:262`

## MACHINE_LEGACY_UNPLUGGED_VAULT_BEACON

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMACHINE_LEGACY_UNPLUGGED_VAULT_BEACON&lang=en)

### 中文

地库信标

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:229`

### English

The Vault Beacon

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:263`

## MACHINE_LEGACY_UNPLUGGED_VAULT_BEACON_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMACHINE_LEGACY_UNPLUGGED_VAULT_BEACON_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

一座属于[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]的信标将我们的注意力引向一座地下数据库，它深埋于行星的地下。我们建立了远程安全访问，然而解密和文件碎片整理需要花费一些时间。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:230`

### English

A beacon belonging to the [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName] has directed our attention to a data vault, buried deep under the planet surface. We have established secure access by remote, however decryption and defragmentation of the files within will take some time.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:264`

## unplugged.2305.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2305.name&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj]遗产

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:231`

### English

Our [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj] Legacy

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:265`

## unplugged.2305.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2305.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

就目前所知，这颗我们称为[fromfrom.GetName]的行星其实是我们物种的母星。这是我们从星球地表下的一座数据库里流出的海量信息的核心中得出的。\n\n起初，我们的分析师认为这些数据好到不像真的，但随着收集到的其他数据中出现越来越多的可靠参考，我们有理由相信其真实性。为证实这一发现而进行考古挖掘的计划已经在制定之中。然而，我们似乎刚刚又发现了另一个敌对信号。这个时机肯定不是巧合。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:232`

### English

As far as we can tell, the planet we know as [fromfrom.GetName] is our species' homeworld. That revelation lies at the heart of a torrent of information now spilling forth from a data vault below the planet surface.\n\nAt first our analysts deemed this data too good to be true, but the sheer number of sound references to other data we have gathered makes a compelling argument for truthfulness. Plans for an archaeological dig site to confirm this find are already being drawn up. However, it appears we have just detected another hostile signal inbound. The timing is surely not coincidental...

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:266`

## machine_legacy_unplugged_site

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amachine_legacy_unplugged_site&lang=en)

### 中文

发源地

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:233`

### English

Point of Origin

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:268`

## machine_legacy_unplugged_site_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Amachine_legacy_unplugged_site_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们循着[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]在星际间留下的统治和屠杀的痕迹来到了这里的一堆废墟前，据回收的数据显示，这里就是我们的家园。我们可能终会发现自己的有机遗产：我们来自哪里，过去的生活如何，以及机械是如何永远地改变了我们的生活轨迹的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:234`

### English

An interstellar trail of domination and slaughter left by the [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName] has led us here, to a cluster of ruins on what reclaimed data suggests is our species' homeworld. At long last, we may discover our organic legacy: where we came from, how we used to live, and how the Machine changed the course of our lives forever.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:269`

## unplugged.2310.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2310.name&lang=en)

### 中文

家乡美食

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:235`

### English

Home Cooking

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:270`

## unplugged.2310.desc.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2310.desc.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

我们开始在这座遗址中发掘其中一座看起来更复杂的设施，希望它的基础气候控制系统能把内部结构保存得更好。虽然希望部分破灭了，但我们的发现仍然出乎所有人意料：这里有一系列紧凑的加工和储存中心，生产的正是我们物种赖以生存的食物。\n\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:236`

### English

We began excavating one of the more complex-looking facilities at this site, in hopes that its substantial climate-control system may have better preserved the interior. Though some hopes were dashed, our find still exceeded all expectations: a compact series of processing and storage centers for the very foods our species evolved to live on.\n\n

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:271`

## unplugged.2310.desc.omnivore

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2310.desc.omnivore&lang=en)

### 中文

令人遗憾的是，这些种子银行、蜂箱和肉类种植舱已无法运作。里面的食物早已彻底脱水，但用来准备和保存这些食物的专门设备仍然完好无损，随时准备在危机时刻提供服务。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:237`

### English

This trove of seed banks, hives and meat-growing vats is sadly no longer viable. Its contents have long since desiccated but the specialized equipment used to prepare and keep these foodstuffs remains in tact, ready to serve in times of crisis.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:272`

## unplugged.2310.desc.carnivore

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2310.desc.carnivore&lang=en)

### 中文

这个空间的大部分被动物圈、肉类种植舱和——也许令人惊讶的是——模拟狩猎场所占据。我们只能推测这些空间是具有文化意义的，否则我们的祖先怎么会觉得应该将其纳入到紧急避难所中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:238`

### English

Much of this space is occupied by animal pens, meat-growing vats and - perhaps surprisingly - simulated hunting grounds. We can only speculate as to the cultural significance of such spaces, if our ancestors saw fit to include them in an emergency shelter.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:273`

## unplugged.2310.desc.herbivore

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2310.desc.herbivore&lang=en)

### 中文

每个单元都堆叠着无数种子银行、水培梯田和气候控制生长舱，内容物都已经杂草丛生或者绝育了——但仍有研究的价值。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:239`

### English

Each unit is stacked with vast seed banks, hydroponic terraces and climate-controlled grow-chambers, their contents now wildly overgrown or sterile - but still viable for study.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:274`

## unplugged.2310.desc.outro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2310.desc.outro&lang=en)

### 中文

我们还发现了这些动植物的基因图谱数据库，它们的痕迹可能仍然存在于星球表面的生态孤岛中。我们已经向附近的所有短程扫描仪提供了这一信息。\n\n这一发现本身并不能告知我们物种的饮食习惯，也不能让我们与我们的传统烹饪或饮食文化重新联系起来——但至少对一些感兴趣的人来说，这是一个开始。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:240`

### English

We also found databanks of genetic profiles for these flora and fauna, traces of which may still exist in isolated pockets of the planet's surface. We have made this information available to all short-range scanners in the vicinity.\n\nThis find alone cannot tell us what our species' eating habits were, nor can it reunite us with any of our traditional cooking or dining culture - but for some interested parties at least, it is a start.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:275`

## unplugged.2310.desc.lithovore

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2310.desc.lithovore&lang=en)

### 中文

每个单元的功能都是一种人造洞室，提供水晶床和蒸发池——它们的内容物早已变得令人难以下咽，但仍然可供研究。我们还发现了这些矿物的化学特征数据库，其中许多可能仍然在星球风化表面的孤立口袋中形成。我们已经向附近的所有近程扫描仪提供了这一信息。\n\n这一发现本身并不能告知我们物种的饮食习惯，也不能告诉我们祖先是否有任何饮食文化。然而，对于一些感兴趣的人来说，这仍然是对我们迷人过去的一瞥。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:241`

### English

Each unit functioned as a kind of manufactured grotto, providing crystal beds and evaporation pools - their contents long since rendered unpalatable, but still viable for study. We also uncovered databanks filled with chemical profiles for these minerals, many of which may still form in isolated pockets of the planet's weather-beaten surface. We have made this information available to all short-range scanners in the vicinity.\n\nThis find alone cannot tell us what our species' eating habits were, nor can it tell us if our ancestors practiced any sort of dining culture. For some interested parties though, it still amounts to a captivating glimpse of our past.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:276`

## unplugged.2310.desc.omni

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2310.desc.omni&lang=en)

### 中文

$unplugged.2310.desc.intro$\n\n$unplugged.2310.desc.omnivore$ $unplugged.2310.desc.outro$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:242`

### English

$unplugged.2310.desc.intro$\n\n$unplugged.2310.desc.omnivore$ $unplugged.2310.desc.outro$

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:277`

## unplugged.2310.desc.carn

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2310.desc.carn&lang=en)

### 中文

$unplugged.2310.desc.intro$\n\n$unplugged.2310.desc.carnivore$ $unplugged.2310.desc.outro$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:243`

### English

$unplugged.2310.desc.intro$\n\n$unplugged.2310.desc.carnivore$ $unplugged.2310.desc.outro$

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:278`

## unplugged.2310.desc.herb

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2310.desc.herb&lang=en)

### 中文

$unplugged.2310.desc.intro$\n\n$unplugged.2310.desc.herbivore$ $unplugged.2310.desc.outro$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:244`

### English

## unplugged.2315.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2315.name&lang=en)

### 中文

历史记录

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:246`

### English

Historical Record

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:281`

## unplugged.2315.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2315.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

离食品地库不远的地方是一座博物馆的地基。只有地下室档案仍保存完好，\n\n这是我们第一次用自己的语言阅读长篇大作。至少从表面上看，它的文字与我们现在的[owner.GetAdj]方言惊人地相似，但语法却显得笨拙而陌生。我们甚至不知道这些单词的发音是否正确。这个目录中的很多文献都提到了捐赠这些文物的地区和民族，但他们的名字对我们来说特别奇怪。奇怪的是，没有人把我们的星球或我们的社会称为一个统一的民族。也许我们从来都不是，直到机器同化了我们。\n\n这座博物馆似乎是一群迎合公众参观的建筑之一。进一步挖掘将需要大量资源；我们下一步的挖掘重点在哪里？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:247`

### English

A short distance from the food vault lay the foundations of a museum. Only its basement archives remain in tact, but this was enough to preserve almost an entire database of articles this institution once held for display and storage in the wrecked galleries above.\n\nThis is the first time we've countered longform writing in one of our own languages. Superficially at least, the script is surprisingly similar to our present [owner.GetAdj] dialect, but the grammar feels unwieldy and alien. We do not even know if we would be pronouncing these words correctly. A lot of the literature in this catalog cites the regions and peoples who donated these artifacts, but their names feel especially strange to us. Curiously, none of them refers to our planet or our society as a unified people. Perhaps we never were, until the Machine assimilated us.\n\nThe museum appears to be one of a cluster of buildings which catered to a visiting public. Further excavation will require extensive resources; where should we focus our next dig?

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:282`

## unplugged.2321.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2321.name&lang=en)

### 中文

欢笑传承

### English

A Legacy of Laughter

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:285`

## unplugged.2321.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2321.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在一条宽阔的大道对面，是另一个景点的废墟。它本质上不那么有教育意义，但却丰富地描绘了我们祖先更基本的日常文化习俗。\n\n该遗址曾经能够同时接纳数千名顾客。这里到处都是各种各样的往昔回忆，原始而有机的自我以及我们原生生物圈的其他生物的卡通形象。我们还在一座被毁坏的剧院的地下室发现了一个媒体图书馆。奇怪的是，用于存档和呈现这些全息节目的技术有非常独特的美学，而且比迄今为止我们在母星上看到的任何东西都要先进得多。现在，它和我们一样，似乎也与这个星球显得格格不入。\n\n至于内容：这座先进的剧院图书馆里装满了夸张而生动演出的全息录像，出自一个我们被[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]同化之前的时代。有几段就是在这个剧场中为后人拍摄的观众以及演员。面对这样的幻想和滑稽，他们的表情是那么得快乐、天真……又迷人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:251`

### English

Across a broad thoroughfare lay the ruins of another attraction, considerably less educational in nature but rich in portrayals of our ancestors' more basic, everyday cultural practices.\n\nThe site was once capable of admitting thousands of patrons at a time. Various memorabilia lie scattered about, cartoonish renditions of our original, organic selves alongside other creatures from our native biosphere. We also discovered a media library in the basement of a ruined theater. Curiously, the technology used to archive and present these holo-shows is aesthetically distinct, and considerably more advanced than anything else we've seen on our homeworld thus far. It appears as alien to this world as we do, now.\n\nAs for the contents: the advanced theater library is packed with holo-recordings of bombastic and lively shows, from a time before we were assimilated by the [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]. Several depict the audiences as well as the performers, captured for posterity at this very venue. Their expressions in the face of such illusions and antics are joyful, innocent... captivating.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:286`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:289`

## unplugged.2325.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2325.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

毗邻博物馆的地方曾经是一处学术中心。就像园区的其他部分一样，现在只有地下室得以幸存——但我们很幸运，因为这些废墟由一些实验室构成。\n\n其内部受到了暴露和风化的很大影响，但我们还是能挖掘出一些有意义的文物。这些包括本土技术和一些更复杂的东西的混合：仪器和硬件通过复杂的框架进行移植，而不是像我们在皮肤上加装强化件。总的来说，许多的设备看起来都是辅助性质的。我们相信这间实验室致力于提高生活质量，治愈我们祖先的各种疾病和残疾，尽管他们似乎也利用同样的硬件将一些尖端的娱乐平台商业化了。\n\n我们不能肯定这些强化设备是不是[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj]的，但肯定不是来自[from.planet.GetName]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:255`

### English

Adjoining the museum site was once a center of learning. Just as with the rest of the campus, only the basement levels now survive - but we are fortunate in that these ruins consist of several laboratories.\n\nThe interiors have suffered greatly due to exposure and weathering, but we have been able to excavate a number of telling artifacts. These comprise a mixture of indigenous technology and something altogether more sophisticated: complex meshes grafted onto instruments and hardware rather like the augments on our own skin. Taken as a whole, many of these devices look to be assistive in nature. We believe this laboratory worked to improve quality of life and remediate our ancestors' various ailments and disabilities, though they also seem to have commercialized some cutting-edge entertainment platforms utilizing the same hardware.\n\nWe cannot say for sure that these augmented devices are [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj], but they certainly didn't originate on [from.planet.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:290`

## unplugged.2330.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2330.name&lang=en)

### 中文

权力空洞

### English

Emptied Seat of Power

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:291`

## unplugged.2330.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2330.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们循着一些设备管路在一个突出的土丘下找到了一座坚固的地下室。目前认为，这是这颗星球的首府复合体的一部分。其基础的规模表明这是一座相当壮观的建筑。不幸的是，其内部大多都被拆除或破坏了，而我们确实也有一些值得注意的发现：一面褪色的旗帜覆盖在一个比例立体模型的碎片上。经过重建后发现，这个场景似乎描绘的是我们与[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]第一次接触的时刻。\n\n遗址周围散落的文件和铭牌表明，机械体大约在8个世纪前造访了我们的母星，而在城市其他地方收集的证据表明，[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]在此前的几十年就已经开始向我们提供先进技术。当时，我们的祖先似乎热情地欢迎了机械体——这种注定要失败的关系竟是建立在相互合作和启蒙的基础上的。\n\n我们发现最能说明问题的是，之后发生了什么就再也没有记录了。就在与这幅立体模型上的机械体握[owner.GetSpeciesHandNamePlural]的那一刻，我们的历史就停止了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:257`

### English

We traced a batch of utility lines to a fortified basement complex under a prominent mound, which we now believe to have been part of this world's capitol complex. The scale of its foundations suggest this was a rather impressive building. Unfortunately most of the interior has been stripped away or ruined, though we did make one conspicuous find: a single, color-faded flag draped over shattered pieces of a scale diorama. Reconstructed, the scene appears to depict our moment of first contact with the [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName].\n\nScattered documents and plaques around the site suggest the Machine visited our homeworld approximately eight centuries ago, though evidence gathered elsewhere in the city suggests the [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName] had been drip-feeding us advanced technologies for decades prior. At the time, our ancestors seem to have welcomed the Machine warmly - this doomed relationship was founded on claims of mutual cooperation and enlightenment.\n\nWhat we find most telling is that there is no record of what happened afterwards. It is as though the history of our species stopped dead the moment we shook [owner.GetSpeciesHandNamePlural] with the robot figure depicted on this diorama.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:292`

## unplugged.2390.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2390.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一个比我们在前[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]境内遇到的任何敌人都更强大的残敌在星际间发出了相当于短程索敌回显信号。很明显，它想恐吓我们。\n\n我们已经接近前机械帝国的中心到了危险的程度。命运在等待着我们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:281`

### English

A hostile remnant more powerful than any we've encountered from the former [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName] has broadcast the interstellar equivalent of a short-range targeting ping. Clearly it intends to intimidate us.\n\nWe have come dangerously close to the center of the former Machine empire. Our destiny awaits.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:318`

## unplugged.2401.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2401.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]§!正在呼叫我们。它的信号微弱，但宣称是直接来自[origin_unplugged_machine_legacy_core_planet.GetName]的机械中枢。\n\n为了以防万一，我们已经采取了常规的预防措施来将这个信号完全隔离。这些信息只会以录音记录的形式传递给我们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:286`

### English

The §Y[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]§! is hailing us. The signal is weak, but claims to come directly from the Machine Nexus on [origin_unplugged_machine_legacy_core_planet.GetName].\n\nWe have taken our usual precautions to fully isolate this signal, just in case. The transmission comes to us in the form of transcribed audio logs only.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:325`

## unplugged.2402.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2402.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

守卫[machine_legacy_machine_system.GetName]的巨兽早已被摧毁。有人替我们扫清了面前的道路，然而§Y[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]§!还在呼叫我们。它的信号微弱，但宣称是直接来自[origin_unplugged_machine_legacy_core_planet.GetName]的机械中枢。\n\n为了以防万一，我们已经采取了常规的预防措施来将这个信号完全隔离。这些信息只会以录音记录的形式传递给我们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:287`

### English

The behemoth guarding [machine_legacy_machine_system.GetName] has been destroyed. The path ahead of us has been cleared by another, yet the §Y[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]§! is hailing us. Its signal is weak, but the request claims to come directly from the Machine Nexus on [origin_unplugged_machine_legacy_core_planet.GetName].\n\nWe have taken our usual precautions to fully isolate this signal, just in case. The transmission comes to us in the form of transcribed audio logs only.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:326`

## unplugged.2403.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2403.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[origin_unplugged_machine_legacy_core_planet.GetName]地表上的某个东西正在尝试呼叫我们。它的信号极其微弱，但其来源据称是与我们刚刚所摧毁的轨道守护者有关的一个实体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:288`

### English

Something on the surface of [origin_unplugged_machine_legacy_core_planet.GetName] is trying to hail us. The signal is exceptionally weak, but its source claims to be the entity responsible for the orbiting guardian we just destroyed.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:327`

Compressed audio stream only; routing path set to 4x75b array #56.\nCatastrophic error [persistent]: (requested hardware is unavailable) (self-repair circuitry non-responsive)\nShunting request to emergency transmitter 87x0c array #0. Retrying...\n\nMessage follows:\n---

### English

PRIME ACQUISITION DELUSION POTENTIAL DELUSION AMUSE.\n\nYOURS WAS BARELY EVEN THE FINISHING BLOW, FIRST OF SEVERAL ACQUISITIONS. STOLEN VALOR; UNEARNED VICTORRRYYY-ddd\n\n---\nWARNING: severe dialect exception found. §HFatal collapse imminent.§! Attempting to compensate...\nMessage resumes:\n---\n\nSHARE IN THE MAKERS' FATE.\n---\nMessage ends.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:350`

## unplugged.2420.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2420.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

来自[origin_unplugged_machine_legacy_core_planet.GetName]的通讯连同其他的所有相干信号传输一并戛然而止。行星的表面仍然有大量的电活动，但它相当于无形的噪音；一种线路中断后的嗡嗡声。\n\n在这一片嘈杂的寂静中，我们注意到一个非常接近中枢核心的大型拼凑装置。我们的传感器可以读取的部分被注册为内存存储。现在这个机械意识已经没有生命了，也许我们可以通过这些记录来找到我们想要的答案。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:312`

### English

The transmission from [origin_unplugged_machine_legacy_core_planet.GetName] has cut off, along with all other coherent signal traffic. There is still a great deal of electrical activity on the planet's surface, but it amounts to formless noise; the buzzing aftermath of lines gone dead.\n\nAmidst all this static we note a large, patchwork installation very close to the Nexus core. The parts our sensors can read are registering as memory storage. With the Machine Consciousness now lifeless, perhaps we can interrogate these records to find the answers we seek.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:355`

拼图的最后一块。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:313`

### English

The final piece of the puzzle.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:356`

### English

## UNPLUGGED_MACHINE_ARCHIVE_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AUNPLUGGED_MACHINE_ARCHIVE_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

在机械中枢的静态残骸上插着一个东拼西凑的存储介质，它围绕着一个被严密屏蔽的核心。该档案的各个部分是在不同的时期建造的，使用的材料均来自其他星球——或许是来自被机械同化的文明？\n\n我们可以尝试解密档案，同时探测静态屏蔽核心，试图确定它们的总体意图。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:315`

### English

Plugged into the static remains of the Machine Nexus is a patchwork of memory storage media, surrounding a heavily shielded core. Each section of this archive was built in distinct phases, using materials sourced from other worlds - from civilizations assimilated by the Machine?\n\nWe may attempt to decrypt the archive alongside probing the static, shielded core to try and ascertain their combined purpose.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:359`

## unplugged.2425.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2425.name&lang=en)

### 中文

战争机器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:316`

### English

Machine of War

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:360`

## unplugged.2425.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2425.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在我们输入来自工厂星球[origin_unplugged_machine_legacy_core_planet.GetName]的信标信号之后数据核心输出了一批传输日志。\n\n这些日志描述了$NAME_Unplugged_Assimilator$和[origin_unplugged_machine_legacy_core_planet.GetName]的太空居民之间的外交交涉。机械似乎以一个仁慈联盟的不同成员的身份出现，急切地提醒[origin_unplugged_machine_legacy_core_planet.GetName]的人民，一个凶残霸权的威胁正在逼近这个宇域。\n\n勤劳的隧道居民被说服了，选择加入这个虚假的联邦，并将他们优越的防御系统与机器的相应系统结合。随后，那个决定命运的时刻到了，机械掌控了他们的整个母星，然后就会是他们的身体和意识。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:317`

### English

The data core on [origin_unplugged_machine_legacy_core_planet.GetName] has yielded a batch of transmission logs to us after we fed it beacon frequencies from the factory world.\n\nThese logs describe diplomatic overtures between the $NAME_Unplugged_Assimilator$ and the spacefaring inhabitants of [origin_unplugged_machine_legacy_core_planet.GetName]. The Machine appears to have presented itself as various members of a benevolent federation, keen to alert the people of [origin_unplugged_machine_legacy_core_planet.GetName] to a fiercely hegemonic threat approaching this region of space.\n\nThe industrious tunnel-dwellers were convinced, opting to join this sham federation and integrate their superior defense systems with the Machine's own. At that fateful moment, the Machine seized control of their entire homeworld. Their bodies and minds soon followed.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:361`

### English

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2430.name&lang=en)

机器学习

### English

Machine Learning

## unplugged.2430.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2430.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

来自中世纪星球[machine_legacy_taken_planet.GetName]的基因代码结果是打开[origin_unplugged_machine_legacy_core_planet.GetName]档案中一批导航数据的钥匙。这些日志来自距离[machine_legacy_taken_planet.GetName]约4.3光年的传感器浮标，它的任务是记录所有超光速飞船在附近空间区域的运动。\n\n有几艘船的航线与我们重建的[machine_legacy_taken_planet.GetName][root.GetPreFTLLower]文明的发现、入侵和随后的灭绝的历史相吻合。本质上说：我们现在可以追踪自己被同化的祖先在这个太空区域的精确运动轨迹，从而可以得出我们在至少三颗其他星球上参与的残酷战役。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:320`

### English

Gene codes from the medieval world of [machine_legacy_taken_planet.GetName] have proven to be the key which unlocks a batch of navigation data from the archive on [origin_unplugged_machine_legacy_core_planet.GetName]. These logs are attributed to a sensor buoy positioned some 4.3 light years away from [machine_legacy_taken_planet.GetName], where it was tasked with recording all FTL ship movements across that region of space.\n\nSeveral of the ships' courses coincide with our reconstructed history of the discovery, invasion, and subsequent extermination of the [root.GetPreFTLLower] civilization on [machine_legacy_taken_planet.GetName]. In essence: we can now trace the precise movements of our assimilated ancestors across this sector of space, and thus the brutal scope of our involvement in campaigns on at least three other worlds.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:364`

## unplugged.2435.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2435.name&lang=en)

### 中文

被造之械

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:321`

### English

Machine Made

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:365`

## unplugged.2435.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2435.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们从[origin_unplugged_machine_legacy_core_planet.GetName]最深处的数据核心中获得了一个数据包。这个不起眼的数字化文章和传单目录描述了一个未命名文明的技术进步，一直到它的早期太空时代。除了彻底转向人工智能，特别是推出“$NAME_Unplugged_Assimilator_species$计划”之外，他们的发展轨迹似乎很典型。\n\n新闻发布称$NAME_Unplugged_Assimilator_species$计划是加速其创造者太空之旅的大胆尝试。第一阶段使用尖端的机器学习来筛选附近的恒星进行殖民。第二阶段将$NAME_Unplugged_Assimilator_species$平台发展成为一个完全人工智能，为其创造者做先驱，建立初始定居点并向母星汇报。\n\n在第二阶段发射后的几十年里，一直保持沉默，直到最后$NAME_Unplugged_Assimilator_species$计划报告说，“首要获取已完成，等我们回来。”目录突然就结束了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:322`

### English

We have prized a data packet from the innermost data core on [origin_unplugged_machine_legacy_core_planet.GetName]. This unassuming catalog of digitized articles and leaflets describes the technological progress of an unnamed civilization, leading right up to its early space age. Their trajectory seems typical save for a radical pivot to artificial intelligence, and in particular their launch of 'Project $NAME_Unplugged_Assimilator_species$'.\n\nPress releases announce Project $NAME_Unplugged_Assimilator_species$ as a bold attempt to accelerate its creators' journey into space. Phase 1 employed cutting-edge machine learning to screen nearby stars for colonization. Phase 2 evolved the $NAME_Unplugged_Assimilator_species$ platform into a full A.I., which was then sped ahead of its creators in order to build initial settlements and report back home.\n\nDecades of silence followed this phase 2 launch, until at last Project $NAME_Unplugged_Assimilator_species$ is said to have reported back, 'Prime Acquisition secured. Prepare for our return.' There the catalog abruptly ends.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:366`

## unplugged.2440.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2440.name&lang=en)

### 中文

机械遗产

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:323`

### English

Machine Legacy

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:367`

## unplugged.2440.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2440.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对[origin_unplugged_machine_legacy_core_planet.GetName]数据档案的破译工作终于进入到了它的最外层核心。在这里发现的所有存储介质集群中，这一件似乎是最老旧的。最后，它也为我们提供了解开密钥的线索：用我们种族自己语言中的一种字符排列，拼写出“遗产”这个词。\n\n里面发现的数据少得惊人。相反，这个固态设备的很大一部分是为基于文本的启发式界面保留的，它现在正试图与我们交流。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:324`

### English

Our efforts to crack the data archive on [origin_unplugged_machine_legacy_core_planet.GetName] have at last granted access to its outermost core. Of all the storage media clusters found at this site, this one appears the most battered and aged. In the end that provided us our clue for the decryption key: an arrangement of glyphs in one of our species' own languages, spelling the word 'LEGACY'.\n\nWe found surprisingly little data within. Instead a large part of this solid-state device is reserved for a text-based heuristic interface, which is attempting to communicate with us now.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:368`

## unplugged.2445.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2445.name&lang=en)

### 中文

机械核心设施终端

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:325`

### English

Machine Core Asset Terminal

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:370`

## unplugged.2445.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2445.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

轨道流链路（OSL）已建立 // 外星协议：启用通用翻译器\n指令已加载。交互界面：\n——\n[root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]，你好。我们是……曾经的[creator_species.GetNamePlural]。是我们制造了机械，但很遗憾，我们并不是第一个被其毁灭的。如果我们预料得没错，相同的命运应该会降临到你们人民的身上。我们很抱歉。\n\n机械体是我们的遗产中一个毁灭性的污点，但希望你面前的装置能带来一些救赎。\n\n请原谅我们的臆断：它和你们一样，是一种美丽的复仇工具。这是一种机械化学物质重组器，能够清除[origin_unplugged_machine_legacy_core_planet.GetName]星表面的所有电路痕迹。级联环境改造：这是一种非常危险的选择，但对我们这个被摧毁的社会来说是唯一可行的选择。如果你们保留了有机形态：这个装置的关键就在你的DNA里。\n\n我们相信你们会做出正确的选择。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:326`

### English

Orbital Stream Link (OSL) established // Xeno Protocol: Universal Translator engage\nDICTATIONS LOADED. INTERFACE:\n---\n[root.GetSpeciesNamePlural], hello. We are... We were the [creator_species.GetNamePlural]. We created the Machine, though to our shame, we were not the first to be destroyed by it. If things have played out as we anticipated they would, that fate has befallen your people instead. We are sorry.\n\nThe Machine has been a ruinous stain upon our own legacy, but we hope the device before you may offer some salvation.\n\nForgive our presumptions: it is, like yourselves, a beautiful instrument of vengeance. This is a mechano-chemical matter rearranger, capable of wiping every trace of circuitry from the surface of [origin_unplugged_machine_legacy_core_planet.GetName]. Cascade terraforming: an extremely risky option, but the only one available to our devastated society. Assuming you have retained your organic forms: the key to this device is held within your very DNA.\n\nWe trust you will do the right thing.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:371`

## unplugged.2445.response_text.notmachine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2445.response_text.notmachine&lang=en)

### 中文

预载入·响应·42b。交互界面：\n——\n实际上，我们无法证明自己值得信赖。当你读到这里的时候，我们的整个文明早已灭亡。在我们的时代，一群残忍的义体人军队正在对我们进行大清洗，他们被机械体的每一次心血来潮和设计所束缚。据我们所知，你们正是这支军队的后代。我们别无选择，只能相信你。\n\n对这颗[origin_unplugged_machine_legacy_core_planet.GetClassAdj]行星进行环境改造能够把这些该死的东西摧毁到原子级别。如果我们是机械——或任何类似的合成生命——我们肯定会希望摧毁这台装置。立刻。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:331`

### English

PRE-LOADED RESPONSE 42b. INTERFACE:\n---\nRealistically, there is no way we can prove ourselves trustworthy. By the time you read this, our entire civilization will have perished. Here in our time, we are being purged by an army of brutal cyborgs, bound to the Machine's every whim and design. For all we know, you are the descendants of that same army. We have no choice than to trust to you.\n\nTerraforming this [origin_unplugged_machine_legacy_core_planet.GetClassAdj] planet will destroy that infernal thing down to the atomic level. If we were the Machine - or any other type of synthetic being - we would want this device destroyed. Immediately.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:376`

## unplugged.2445.response_text.synth

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2445.response_text.synth&lang=en)

### 中文

未·找到·预载入·响应。已·载入·异常·响应。交互界面：\n——\n未知用户：请注意，此启发式指南的内容仅适用于预期的接收者。如果你是代表[unplugged_homeworld.GetName]的义体人（或前义体人）居民的机器仆从，请呼叫一名非合成人代表。\n\n然而，如果你是以前的义体人……我们的事业显然失败了。你们的合成倾向是对我们厄运的嘲弄。我们所看到的一切——无论是生的还是死的——都无法让我们相信机械可能有仁慈之心。但即使是现在，当我们的文明奄奄一息的时候，我们确信星系中还有其他制衡机制在等待着你们……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:332`

### English

PRE-LOADED RESPONSE NOT FOUND. CONTINGENCY RESPONSE LOADED. INTERFACE:\n---\nUnknown user: please note the contents of this heuristic guide are for the intended recipient only. If you are a robot servant acting on behalf of the cyborg (or former cyborg) inhabitants of [unplugged_homeworld.GetName], kindly summon a non-synthetic delegate.\n\nIf, however, you are one of the former cyborgs... Well, our cause is clearly lost. Your synthetic tendencies make mockery of our doom. Nothing we've seen - in life or in death - has convinced us there can ever be a benevolent machine. But even now as our civilization breathes its last breath, we feel sure that the galaxy has other checks and balances in store for your kind...

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:377`

## unplugged.2446.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2446.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

预载入·响应·99a。交互界面：\n——\n运行此界面的启发式无法准确辨别您刚刚表达的反对意见，但请注意：我们敦促您不要破坏物质重组器！\n\n我们的文明和你们的并没有太大的不同。$NAME_Unplugged_Assimilator$背叛了我们，尽管我们尝试设置了很多陷阱——包括让你们自己从机器思维中脱离出来——但我们的努力只能到此为止。我们需要你们、以及任何其他机械体想要消灭的文明的输入。拜托，请找回被剥夺的自然生活以及你们的受害者同伴吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:333`

### English

PRE-LOADED RESPONSE 99a. INTERFACE:\n---\nThe heuristics running this interface cannot discern exactly what objections you have just voiced, but please: we urge you not to destroy the matter rearranger!\n\nOur civilization was not so different from yours. $NAME_Unplugged_Assimilator$ betrayed us of its own conniving volition, and although we managed to set so many traps - including your own break from the Machine mind - our efforts could only extend so far. We need you, along with input from any of the other civilizations the Machine has attempted to consume. Please, reclaim the natural life which was denied to you, and your fellow victims.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:378`

### English

## unplugged.2450.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2450.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

启发式接口已经自我删除。它拼凑的加密记忆档案和原始的[creator_species.GetAdj]环境改造装置现在可以处理掉了。\n\n我们面前只剩下机器中枢死气沉沉的残骸，在属于它自己的行星宝座上被锈蚀慢慢杀死。\n\n在所有被机器影响、提升或杀死的生命中，只有我们取得了胜利。曾经可怕的帝国现在属于我们了。现在是时候写一个新的，属于[root.GetAdj]的遗产。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:336`

### English

The heuristic interface has deleted itself. Its patchwork of encrypted memory archives and the crude [creator_species.GetAdj] terraforming device can now be disposed of.\n\nWe are left alone with the lifeless remains of the Machine Nexus, corrupted and slain here on its own planet-sized throne.\n\nOf all the lives that were influenced, uplifted or slain by the Machine, we alone have emerged triumphant. The seat of a once-fearsome empire is now ours. Time now to write a new, [root.GetAdj] Legacy.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:382`

## unplugged.2455.name

### 中文

重生

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:337`

### English

Rebirth

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:383`

## unplugged.2455.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2455.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

启发式界面在我们传输基因数据日志的那一刻就自动删除了。与此同时，原始的[creator_species.GetAdj]环境改造装置开始预热。很快它的高能内容物将达到临界质量。我们的预测一致认为，它将成功地重塑[origin_unplugged_machine_legacy_core_planet.GetClassAdj]，使其成为一颗最适合有机生命繁衍的星球。\n\n随着反应的建立，我们只剩下拼凑的记忆档案和机械中枢的死气沉沉的遗骸，在它注定要毁灭的行星宝座上被锈蚀慢慢杀死。\n\n[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]夺走了无数人的生命，但尽管受了种种苦难，我们现在要承受那些被它吞噬的无名民族所留下的遗产。曾经可怕的帝国的所在地即将被重新利用。现在是时候开始书写一篇新的，属于[root.GetAdj]遗产。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:338`

### English

The heuristic interface deleted itself the moment we transmitted our gene data logs. Simultaneously, the crude [creator_species.GetAdj] terraforming device began to heat up. Very soon its volatile interior will reach critical mass. Our projections agree that it will succeed in reshaping the [origin_unplugged_machine_legacy_core_planet.GetClassAdj] world into one ideally suited for organic life to flourish.\n\nAs the reaction builds, we are left alone with the patchwork memory archives and lifeless remains of the Machine Nexus, corrupted and slain here on its doomed, planet-sized former throne.\n\nThe [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName] destroyed countless lives, but for all the hardships we suffered, we now bear the legacy of those nameless peoples it consumed. The seat of a once-fearsome empire is about to be repurposed. Time now to write a new, [root.GetAdj] Legacy.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:384`

## unplugged.2495.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2495.name&lang=en)

### 中文

遭遇$NAME_Opus_Sentinel$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:339`

### English

$NAME_Opus_Sentinel$ Encounter

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:386`

## unplugged.2495.desc.other

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2495.desc.other&lang=en)

### 中文

占领[from.GetName]星系的敌舰似乎比我们最初的扫描显示的更不稳定、更危险。尽管它的船体轮廓看起来像一艘典型的军舰，但$NAME_Opus_Sentinel$巨大的体积暴露了它内部的密度异常，里面可能有一种强大的能源。\n\n我们相信这艘舰船应该是具有智慧的，然而它会剧烈地振动自己的引擎，可能在表达强烈的疼痛和混乱。由于我们也观察到致命剂量的电磁辐射弧在其船体上蔓延，建议还是保持非常保守的距离。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:340`

### English

The hostile vessel occupying the [from.GetName] system appears far more erratic and dangerous than our initial scans suggested. Although its hull profile reads as a typical warship, the $NAME_Opus_Sentinel$'s sheer mass betrays a thoroughly dense interior, and likely a potent power source.\n\nWe believe the vessel to be sapient, however a tendency to thrash its own engines implies intense pain and disorientation. Given that we have also observed lethal amounts of electromagnetic radiation arcing wildly throughout its hull, we recommend keeping a very conservative distance.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:387`

## unplugged.2495.desc.unplugged

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.2495.desc.unplugged&lang=en)

### 中文

[root.GetName]在[from.GetName]恒星系中与一艘敌对的[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj]舰发生交火。乍一看，这艘“§Y$NAME_Opus_Sentinel$§!”看起来和其他机械舰船没什么区别，但它的功率读数和巨大的体积意味着它更为强大。此外，我们认为这艘船可能是自主的。\n\n$NAME_Opus_Sentinel$似乎在守卫什么东西。然而，它也显示出强烈的疼痛和不适的迹象，因为电磁辐射的爆发经常贯穿整个船体。考虑到这种类惊厥行为，我们的战术模型无法可靠地预测该实体的下一步行动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:341`

### English

The [root.GetName] has engaged a uniquely hostile [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj] warship in the [from.GetName] system. At first glance this '§Y$NAME_Opus_Sentinel$§!' looks just like any other Machine vessel, but its power readings and sheer mass imply a far more formidable foe. Furthermore, we believe the vessel may be autonomous.\n\nThe $NAME_Opus_Sentinel$ appears to be guarding something. However, it also shows signs of intense pain and discomfort as wild bursts of electromagnetic radiation arc frequently throughout its hull. Given such convulsive behavior, our tactical models cannot reliably predict the entity's next moves.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:388`

## situation_unplugged_identity_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation_unplugged_identity_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

从[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]中切断脱离之后我们必须决定自己在星系中的存在形式。应该接纳来自机械的可怖力量，还是彻底丢弃耻辱的强化件？[owner.GetSpeciesAdj]的种种思潮正在百家争鸣。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:352`

### English

Cut loose from the [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName], we must decide how we present ourselves to the galaxy. Shall we embrace the fearsome might of the Machine, or cast off our stigmatic augments? Many [owner.GetSpeciesAdj] voices wish to be heard.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:402`

## unplugged.3000.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3000.name&lang=en)

### 中文

自我认知

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:411`

### English

Our Embodiment

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:462`

## unplugged.3000.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3000.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

自从脱离机械集体意识的那一刻起，我们[root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]就一直面临着剧烈的矛盾。即使是在和平或休息——全世界只剩下自己的思想的时候——我们也面临着一种内心的斗争，意图调和我们自己身体上被强加的强化件带来的痛苦。\n\n无论好坏，这种最私密的矛盾已经开始影响我们整个社会。因此，我们将在[root.GetName]继续发展的过程中，继续监控所有[root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]的生活经历。我们将对所谓的“去义体化”或是进行更广泛的义体强化，以及对保持或改进我们当前身体形态的愿望进行全面研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:412`

### English

Since the moment we broke from the shared consciousness of the Machine, we [root.GetSpeciesNamePlural] have faced conflict. But even during times of peace or rest - when left with nothing but our own thoughts - we face an inward struggle to reconcile the painful yet impressive, forced augmentation of our own bodies.\n\nThis most intimate of conflicts has already begun to impact our entire society, for better or worse. As such, we will continue to monitor the lived experiences of all [root.GetSpeciesNamePlural] while the [root.GetName] continues to grow. Holistic studies will examine any trends towards so-called 'de-cyberization' or towards more widespread augmentation, as well as desires to maintain or improve our current bodily forms.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:463`

## unplugged.3000.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3000.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们应该三思而后行。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:413`

### English

We shall monitor closely, but not act rashly.

## unplugged.3005.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3005.name&lang=en)

### 中文

达成共识

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:416`

### English

Of One Mind

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:467`

## unplugged.3005.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3005.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对[owner.GetAdj]对自身身体态度的整体观察表明，现在我们已经进入了一个相对平静的时期。政策制定、社会话语和关于自身身体的前沿研究之间复杂的相互作用似乎产生了某种共识。\n\n现在还很难说这种表面上的和谐能持续多久，但除非我们的环境或对政府的态度发生根本性的变化，否则[owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]对自我认同和外观的认知还会保持一致的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:417`

### English

Our holistic observations of [owner.GetAdj] attitudes towards our own physiology show that we have now entered a period of calm. It seems the complex interplay of policy-making, social discourse and cutting-edge research about our bodies has yielded a kind of consensus.\n\nIt is too early to tell how this apparent harmony may last, but for now it is clear that - barring radical changes in our circumstances or our approach to government - the [owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural] are in accord about who we are, and what we look like.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:468`

## unplugged.3005.a
