## unplugged.3010.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3010.name&lang=en)

### 中文

意见分歧

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:421`

### English

Divided Opinions

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:472`

## unplugged.3010.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3010.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

正在进行的关于我们[owner.GetAdj]对自己生理学态度的研究表明，我们现在已经进入了一个文化动荡的时期。政策制定、社会话语和关于我们身体的前沿研究之间复杂的相互作用，导致了舆论的剧烈波动。如今，我们的城市中心充满激情的抗议和争论的景象已经为人司空见惯。\n\n这些爆发很少直接导致暴力或骚乱；事实上，一些公民对我们允许如此多的声音被听到感到自豪。然而，同样明显的是，[owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]已经成为一个四分五裂的民族，我们关于自我认同的文化观念前所未有地脆弱。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:422`

### English

Our ongoing study of [owner.GetAdj] attitudes towards our own physiology show that we have entered a period of cultural turmoil. The complex interplay of policy-making, social discourse and cutting-edge research about our bodies has led to wild swings in opinion, with passionate protests and arguments now commonplace in our urban centers.\n\nFew of these outbursts lead directly to violence or civil upset; indeed, some citizens have expressed pride that we allow so many voices to be heard. However, it is also clear that the [owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural] have become a divided people, and our culture's ideas about self-identity are now more fragile than ever.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:473`

## unplugged.3010.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3010.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们需要为这种争论找到一个更好的平台。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:423`

### English

We must find a better forum for these debates.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:474`

## unplugged.3090.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3090.name&lang=en)

### 中文

我们是[owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:424`

### English

We, the [owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:475`

## unplugged.3090.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3090.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

“我们表里如一，不多不少。”\n\n这是在我们的社会与身体的认知斗争中出现的许多朗朗上口的座右铭之一。可以自信地说，我们已经摆脱了许多怀疑、恐惧和不切实际的妄想。\n\n至于我们是否在比如对更广阔的星系外交政策中遵守这样的标准，那就是另一回事了。至少在国内，我们[owner.GetSpeciesName]似乎对身体形态和自己不断走向自我实现的旅程感到满意。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:425`

### English

'We are as we seem; nothing more, nothing less.'\n\nThis is just one of the many simplistic but catchy mottos to have emerged from our society's tussle with bodily identity. We can justifiably claim freedom from many of the doubts, fears, and impossible longings we once had about our embodied selves.\n\nWhether or not we honor such standards as foreign policy for the wider galaxy is another matter. Domestically at least, the [owner.GetSpeciesName] appear satisfied with our physical forms, and our continuing journey towards self-realization.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:476`

## unplugged.3100.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3100.name&lang=en)

### 中文

移除的土壤

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:426`

### English

Grounds for Removal

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:478`

## unplugged.3100.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3100.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

越来越多的[root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]觉得我们应该施行更多举措以远离机械，例如限制或者直接移除强加在我们身上的强化件。\n\n公众要求我们为他们所认为的内在危机找到解决办法，以便改善我们在其他国家面前的声誉，并为许多每天遭受严重耻辱和畸形苦难的[root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]提供救济。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:427`

### English

A growing number of [root.GetSpeciesNamePlural] believe we should do more to distance ourselves from our Machine aspects, by limiting or outright removing the augments that were imposed upon our bodies.\n\nThe public is demanding that we find solutions for what they consider to be a domestic crisis, in order to improve our reputation with foreign powers and provide relief for the many [root.GetSpeciesNamePlural] suffering everyday hardships borne of profound shame and dysmorphia.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:479`

## unplugged.3100.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3100.a&lang=en)

### 中文

绝对不行。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:428`

### English

Out of the question.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:480`

## unplugged.3100.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3100.b&lang=en)

### 中文

会看到它完成的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:429`

### English

We shall see it done.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:481`

## unplugged.3105.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3105.name&lang=en)

### 中文

去义体化突破

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:430`

### English

De-Cyberization Breakthrough

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:483`

## unplugged.3105.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3105.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一家民间实验室中致力于[target.GetName]“去义体化”课题的研究者宣布获得了重大的突破。他们声称开发出了一种可以移除共价纳米框架装甲以及依附其上的关键强化件的科技。\n\n一代又一代的[target.GetSpeciesNamePlural]不得不忍受他们认为的这种笨拙和令人生畏的身体，仅仅是因为这种合成性底层作为我们许多强化件的重要组成部分起着关键的功能。但如今，我们已经可以用自己制造的皮肤强化件来代替这种框架，巧妙地与有机材料交织在一起——换句话说，拥有了真正的[target.GetSpeciesAdj]皮肤。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:431`

### English

Researchers at a civilian laboratory dedicated to the cause of 'de-cyberizing' the [target.GetName] have announced a significant breakthrough. They claim to have developed a technique for removing bonded nanomesh armor along with the vital augments that depend on it.\n\nGenerations of [target.GetSpeciesNamePlural] have had to suffer what they consider to be unwieldy and intimidating physical forms, merely because this synthetic underlay is an essential component of many of our augmented, vital functions. Now though, we may replace that mesh with a dermal augment of our own making, cleverly interwoven with organic material - in other words, a true [target.GetSpeciesAdj] skin.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:484`

## unplugged.3105.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3105.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这些装甲保护了我们一代又一代。应该保留。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:432`

### English

That armor has protected us for generations. It stays.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:485`

## unplugged.3106.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3106.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们自己对[target.GetName]去义体化的努力取得了另一项突破，医疗机构现在请求大量应用一种激进的新手术形式。专家称，各种生物机械依赖的解决方法已经找到了，此前这种情况甚至会影响到新生的[target.GetSpeciesNamePlural]，迫使其要配备一套可回收的模块化增强部件。\n\n一个跨学科的审查小组已经审查了这一流程，并补充说，移除这些模块化武器端口也将减轻维修这些外来强化件的经济负担。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:433`

### English

Our homegrown effort to de-cyberize the [target.GetName] has made another breakthrough, with medical facilities now petitioning to scale up a radical new form of surgery. Experts claim to have found workarounds for the myriad bio-mechanical dependencies that afflict even newborn [target.GetSpeciesNamePlural], forcing them to be fitted with a suite of recycled, modular augments.\n\nA cross-disciplinary review panel has already vetted the process, adding that removing these modular weapon ports will also reduce the economic burden of servicing such alien augments.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:486`

## unplugged.3106.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3106.a&lang=en)

### 中文

拒绝。这些模块让我们在战斗中受益匪浅。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:434`

### English

Denied. Those modules give us our edge in combat.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:487`

## unplugged.3107.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3107.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

许多[target.GetSpeciesName]的梦想即将实现，研究人员终于找到了一种恢复重建我们自身的有机神经系统的方法。他们称，一种基因疗法和固件破解的新组合可以让测试对象在不依赖强化神经分流、而是利用自身的运动反射的情况下运作良好。初步研究甚至表明，这些疗法可能会减少甚至根除我们自从与机器意识分离以来所遭受的无数感官幻觉。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:435`

### English

The dream of many a [target.GetSpeciesName] nears realization, as researchers have at last found a way to revitalize and restore our bodies' own organic nervous systems. They claim that a novel blend of genetic therapies and firmware hacking has allowed test subjects to thrive without relying on augmented nerve shunts, instead utilizing their own motor reflexes. Preliminary studies even suggest these therapies may reduce or eradicate the myriad sensory hallucinations we all suffer since we were severed from the machine consciousness.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:488`

## unplugged.3107.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3107.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们可能还是需要这些强化战斗反射。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:436`

### English

We may need those enhanced combat reflexes.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:489`

## unplugged.3110.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3110.name&lang=en)

### 中文

机械同调

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:437`

### English

Machine Harmonization

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:491`

## unplugged.3110.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3110.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一个致力于控制我们的强化件的民间研究小组发现了增强无线[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj]网络的方法，该网络曾经用于同步我们的所有强化件，一旦缺失将会因为无法找到设备信号而给[target.GetSpeciesNamePlural]带来无尽的烦恼和痛苦。尽管这种替代网络在设计上是高度集中的——根据定义，它必须以[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetSpeciesAdj]中枢为模型——但我们确信它是安全的、只服务于[target.GetName]的利益。在最初的测试批次中，受试者报告称效率有所提高了，也感到了舒适，是一种相当于幸福的解脱状态。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:438`

### English

A civilian research team dedicated to granting control of our own augments has discovered how to supplement the wireless [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj] mesh which once synchronized all our augments, whose absence has caused no end of irritation and suffering to [target.GetSpeciesNamePlural] unable to ever find a device signal. Although this replacement network is highly centralized by design - by definition it must be modeled on the [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetSpeciesAdj] Nexus - we are assured that it is safe, serving only the interests of the [target.GetName]. Subjects from the initial test batch have reported increased efficiency, comfort, and a state of relief tantamount to bliss.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:492`

## unplugged.3110.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3110.a&lang=en)

### 中文

不。别再搞了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:439`

### English

Never again. No.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:493`

## unplugged.3111.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3111.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们进一步控制自己强化件的努力在义体外科领域取得了突破。一支医学[target.GetScientistPlural]团队在我们身体的模块化接口中成功分离出确切的依赖关系——我们因此可以轻松地更换整个肢体、武器装备或身体框架的机器元素。我们应该就能以此改造现有的身体，并为新生的[target.GetSpeciesNamePlural]安装定制接口，从而减少我们对使用回收的[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj]设备进行拙劣维修的依赖。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:440`

### English

Our efforts to exert further control over our own augments have yielded a breakthrough in the field of cybernetic surgery. A team of medical [target.GetScientistPlural] has isolated the exact dependencies in our bodies' modular interfaces - the machine elements which allow us to swap out entire limbs, weapon rigs, and body frames with ease. This should enable us to retrofit existing bodies and install custom interfaces for newborn [target.GetSpeciesNamePlural], severing our reliance on botched repairs with salvaged [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj] equipment.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:494`

## unplugged.3111.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3111.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这步跨太远了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:441`

### English

This is a step too far.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:495`

## unplugged.3112.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3112.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们终于完全掌握了自己的强化件，因为我们现在拥有了一种突破性的治疗感官幻觉的方法。自从我们从机器意识中分离出来后，绝大多数[target.GetSpeciesNamePlural]都因为自己的潜意识在寻找一种再也无法听见的合唱而遭受了焦虑和抑郁。现在，我们可以通过一种非联网的辅助植入体来抑制这种渴望，它可以模仿机械集体的认知波长。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:442`

### English

Mastery of our own augments is finally within our grasp, as we now possess a breakthrough treatment for sensory hallucinations. Ever since we were severed from the machine consciousness, the vast majority of [target.GetSpeciesNamePlural] have suffered anxiety and depression as they subconsciously seek a chorus that is no longer audible to us. Now though, we may suppress this longing by means of a non-networked, auxiliary implant which mimics the cognitive wavelengths of a machine collective.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:496`

## unplugged.3112.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3112.a&lang=en)

### 中文

假装自己又是一个机械意识了？难以想象。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:443`

### English

Pretending to be a machine mind again? Unthinkable.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:497`

## unplugged.3115.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3115.name&lang=en)

### 中文

环境变形

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:444`

### English

Ambient Dysmorphia

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:499`

## unplugged.3115.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3115.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[target.GetAdj]边缘社会的紧张局势仍在持续发酵，反对者呼吁想方设法采取更多行动摆脱义体强化件。\n\n然而，对于越来越多的公民来说，这场辩论已经进入了一种更平静、更微妙的状态。许多[target.GetSpeciesNamePlural]开始通过化妆来自我满足，选择根据自己的意愿隐藏或突出自己的强化件。这些权宜之计并不令[target.GetSpeciesNamePlural]满意，但仍是勉强可以接受的一种新趋势。\n\n或许公平地说，[target.GetName]正在学会应对普遍的冷漠和不适。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:445`

### English

Tensions continue to simmer on the fringes of [target.GetAdj] society as opposing voices call for more action to address our cybernetic augments, one way or the other.\n\nFor a growing majority of citizens, however, this debate has already settled into something more sedate and nuanced. Many [target.GetSpeciesNamePlural] have sought contentment by leaning into cosmetics, opting to hide or accentuate their augments on their own terms. Few [target.GetSpeciesNamePlural] express actual satisfaction with stopgap measures like these, but they are part of a new trend towards grudging acceptance.\n\nIt is perhaps fair to say the [target.GetName] is learning to cope with general apathy and discomfort.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:500`

## unplugged.3115.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3115.a&lang=en)

### 中文

明白了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:446`

### English

Understood.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:501`

## unplugged.3116.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3116.name&lang=en)

### 中文

维持现状

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:447`

### English

Maintaining the Status Quo

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:502`

## unplugged.3116.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3116.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[target.capital_scope.GetName]上的地方政府报告称，公众示威及和平抗议行为有所增加。以上情况均得到了妥善的监控和处理——更广泛的大众没有受到惊扰。然而，这些骚扰事件均与义体政策相关，因此遭到了渴望维持目前生活方式的公民的抵制。\n\n对于[target.GetName]的人们来说，“现状”这一意识形态似乎并不仅仅是无所作为。相反，现在有很多[target.GetSpeciesNamePlural]正在积极反对“进一步干预”我们的强化件。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:448`

### English

Local authorities on [target.capital_scope.GetName] have reported an increase in public demonstrations and peaceful acts of civil disobedience. Each of these was monitored or dealt with as and when appropriate - the wider population has not experienced any cause for alarm. However, each of these disturbances was linked to issues of cybernetic policy, and as such met with resistance from citizens keen to maintain our current way of life.\n\nIt would seem that for the people of the [target.GetName], the 'status quo' ideology is not merely one of inaction. Rather, there are plenty of [target.GetSpeciesNamePlural] who now actively campaign against 'further meddling' with our augments.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:503`

## unplugged.3116.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3116.a&lang=en)

### 中文

已了解。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:449`

### English

Understood.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:504`

## unplugged.3117.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3117.name&lang=en)

### 中文

事态稳固

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:450`

### English

Solid State of Affairs

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:505`

## unplugged.3117.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3117.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

虽然一些[target.GetSpeciesNamePlural]仍在抱怨政府在义体强化件问题上无所作为，但更广泛的公众舆论已经变得更加坚忍。一些务实的人联合起来为那些最需要的人提供慈善援助和咨询。与此同时，医疗机构已经开始为我们不得不依赖的回收零件建立新的供应链。\n\n缓慢而坚定地，我们的社会正在适应自己的外表以及强加给我们的一系列环境。我们面前还有大量的工作要做，但在这种妥协中存有一丝希望。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:451`

### English

Though several [target.GetSpeciesNamePlural] continue to rail against what they perceive as government inaction in the matter of our cybernetic augments, broader public opinion has settled into something more stoic. A number of pragmatic individuals have banded together to provide charitable aid and counseling for those who need it most. Meanwhile, medical facilities have begun to establish new supply chains for the recycled parts we are forced to rely on.\n\nSlowly but surely, our society is coming to terms with its own appearance, and the set of circumstances imposed upon us. A great deal of work lies ahead of us yet, but there is a faint feeling of hope in this acceptance.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:506`

## unplugged.3117.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3117.a&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管如此这也是个结论。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:452`

### English

A conclusion nonetheless.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:507`

## unplugged.3205.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3205.name&lang=en)

### 中文

恐怖的遗产

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:453`

### English

A Legacy of Terror

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:509`

## unplugged.3205.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3205.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

越来越多的[root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]认为我们最近与[fromfrom.GetName]令人不快的对话暴露了一个可以追溯到我们从[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]中崭露头角之时的尴尬事实：其他人害怕我们。不仅如此，这种恐惧源自机械体，而不是我们的任何行动。这种恐惧具有继承性而不是由我们自己造成的，因此也难以控制。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:454`

### English

A growing number of [root.GetSpeciesNamePlural] believe our recent and uncomfortable dialog with the [fromfrom.GetName] points to an awkward truth, dating back to our emergence from the [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]: others fear us. Moreover, that fear is borne of the Machine, and not our own actions. It is inherited, unearned, and thus may be difficult to control.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:510`

## unplugged.3206.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3206.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

越来越多的[root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]认为饱受战争蹂躏的[fromfrom.GetName]暴露了一个尴尬的事实：其他人害怕我们，而且是出于我们无法控制的原因。\n\n我们的外表往往会引发一种致命的恐怖——如果我们希望与其他物种进行“软”外交，这很难说是什么优点。但也有可能这种残酷的遗产先于我们存在，许多人只是通过我们看到了机械体曾经的凶残，而不是因为我们是[root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]而感到恐惧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:455`

### English

A growing number of [root.GetSpeciesNamePlural] believe the devastation of battle-stricken [fromfrom.GetName] lays bare an awkward truth: others fear us, and for reasons which are outside our control.\n\nOur very appearance tends to provoke a sort of lethal terror - hardly an asset if we should ever wish to engage in 'soft' diplomacy with other species. But it is also possible that our cruel legacy precedes us, in that many perceive only the brutality of the Machine we once were, rather than dealing with us as [root.GetSpeciesNamePlural].

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:511`

## unplugged.3205.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3205.a&lang=en)

### 中文

无论如何，他们理应害怕我们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:456`

### English

They are right to fear us, regardless.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:512`

## unplugged.3205.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3205.b&lang=en)

### 中文

这样不好。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:457`

### English

This will not do.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:513`

## unplugged.3250.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3250.name&lang=en)

### 中文

恐惧机械

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:458`

### English

Fear of the Machine

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:514`

## unplugged.3250.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3250.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

政府官员听到我们与一个机械智能建立外交关系的消息后感到震惊。目前还没有人直接向[fromfrom.GetName]表达不满，但有些人已经在起草建议，希望采取先发制人的制裁和应急措施，防止它们干涉我们的事务。\n\n然而，也有许多[root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]对这一发展表示兴奋。无论他们的态度是出于真正的排外主义，还是对我们自身的机械元素更务实的欣赏，我们都有望能向一个更仁慈的机械智能体学习。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:459`

### English

Government officials are reeling at the news we have opened diplomatic relations with a Machine Intelligence. None have expressed their displeasure directly to the [fromfrom.GetName] yet, but several are already drafting proposals for pre-emptive sanctions and contingencies against their assumed meddling in our affairs.\n\nThere are, however, many [root.GetSpeciesNamePlural] who have expressed excitement at this development. Whether their attitudes are borne of genuine xenophilia or a more pragmatic appreciation for our own Machine elements, there is hope in the notion we might learn from a more benevolent example of machine intelligence.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:515`

## unplugged.3255.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3255.name&lang=en)

### 中文

往昔恐惧

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:460`

### English

Fear of the Past

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:516`

## unplugged.3255.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3255.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们与另一个和[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]如此相似的机械遭遇的消息在外交使团中引发了轩然大波。在场的人自始至终都保持着镇定，目前为止还没有向[fromfrom.GetName]透露出任何特别的弱点或恐惧。然而，现在我们的集体心灵和士气确实面临着严峻的挑战。\n\n最高机密公报已经发给了负责保卫边疆的军事人员。然而，如果我们希望对这次遭遇的消息保密的话这也是一个重大的挑战。有些官员已经询问了是否应该通知[root.GetAdj]人民，让他们有自己的发言权。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:461`

### English

News that we have encountered another Machine so similar to the [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName] has sent shockwaves through our diplomatic corps. Those present have kept their composure throughout, and so far we have not betrayed any particular weakness or fear to the [fromfrom.GetName]. We do, however, now face a serious challenge to our collective psyche and morale.\n\nTop secret communiques have already been dispatched to military personnel tasked with protection of our borders. However, we also face a significant challenge should we wish to keep news of this encounter quiet. Some officials have asked whether or not we should inform the [root.GetAdj] people, and let them have their own say.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:517`

## pre_ftl_remains_site

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apre_ftl_remains_site&lang=en)

### 中文

摇篮废墟

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:462`

### English

The Ruined Cradle

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:519`

## pre_ftl_remains_site_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apre_ftl_remains_site_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.Planet.GetName]的表面遍布系统性毁灭的伤疤：残垣断壁、满目疮痍、一片死寂。最初的扫描显示这里曾经存在过一个繁荣的前超光速社会，直到一些事件导致了他们灾难性的终结。四处散落的遗骸低语着一种无法言说的恐怖。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:463`

### English

The surface of [From.Planet.GetName] planet bears the scars of systematic devastation: blasted landscapes, collapsed cities, and an eerie silence. Initial scans suggest a once-thriving pre-FTL society existed here - until something brought about their catastrophic end. The scattered remains whisper of an untold horror.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:520`

## unplugged.3300.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3300.name&lang=en)

### 中文

挣扎迹象

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:464`

### English

Signs of Struggle

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:522`

## unplugged.3300.desc.unplugged

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3300.desc.unplugged&lang=en)

### 中文

对[From.Planet.GetName]的初步调查揭开了这个残毁世界的面纱。十村九屠、遗物遍野，建筑物上熔蚀着远超前超光速社会生产力的热能武器痕迹。降临于此之物从容不迫地带来了瞬间的毁灭。\n\n这种摧毁行动精确无比，表明这是一场有组织有预谋的进攻。尽管没有看到任何遗体，但证据很明显：他们试图逃离，但根本逃不掉。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:465`

### English

The initial survey of [From.Planet.GetName] reveals a shattered world. Decimated villages, roads littered with abandoned belongings, and structures scorched by heat signatures far beyond what a pre-FTL society could have produced. Whatever descended upon this world brought devastation swiftly and deliberately.\n\nThe precision of the destruction suggests an organized assault. Though no bodies are in sight, the evidence is clear: the population fled, though not far enough.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:523`

## unplugged.3300.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3300.a&lang=en)

### 中文

令人不安。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:466`

### English

Disturbing.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:524`

## unplugged.3305.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3305.name&lang=en)

### 中文

机械残余

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:467`

### English

Machine Remnants

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:526`

## unplugged.3305.desc.unplugged

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3305.desc.unplugged&lang=en)

### 中文

进一步发掘带来了对这场冲突本质的更深层洞见。废墟中四处散落着熔毁的机械外壳——是一种和我们自身的植入体不同的先进义体构造。但和我们类似，它们也不单单是武器；而是战争生存工具，既是机械也是生物。虽然目前下结论还为时尚早，但令人不安的是，这正是我们突然“醒来”的恒星系。\n\n更糟糕的是，战争机器上有几处完好无损的标记——这些令人不安的符号表明，侵略者的真正目的不是征服，而是为了某种更系统性的事情。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:468`

### English

Deeper excavation has provided more insight into the nature of the conflict. Scattered across the ruins are melted husks of machinery - advanced cybernetic constructs not unlike our own implants. Like us, these were not simple weapons; they were living tools of war, simultaneously mechanical and biological. It is too soon to draw conclusions, but it is disturbing to reflect on the fact that this is the very system in which we suddenly "awoke."\n\nWorse still are the few intact markings on the war machines - disturbing symbols that suggest their invader's true goal was not conquest but rather something far more systematic.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:527`

## unplugged.3305.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3305.a&lang=en)

### 中文

问题比答案多多了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:469`

### English

More questions than answers.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:528`

## unplugged.3310.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3310.name&lang=en)

### 中文

沉默信号

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:470`

### English

Silent Signals

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:530`

## unplugged.3310.desc.unplugged

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3310.desc.unplugged&lang=en)

### 中文

我们发现了一座曾经庞大的通讯中心，埋在一座设防城市的废墟下。对于一个前超光速社会来说，这座设施远比预期的先进。更重要的是，它的天线阵列不是为本地广播而设计的——它们是为了向太空深处传输而校准的。\n\n经过艰苦的分析，我们从该系统提取出了它最后发出的信息的片段。在混乱的传输中，有声音请求拯救，绝望地呼唤着某个远在这个恒星系之外的未知坐标。每一次广播都越来越紧迫，似乎行星上的入侵者知道他们正在打一场必败的战争，并把最后的希望寄托在虚空中的某物或某个人身上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:471`

### English

We have uncovered what was once a sprawling communications hub, buried beneath the ruins of a fortified city. This facility was far more advanced than expected for a pre-FTL society. What's more, its antenna arrays were not designed for local broadcasts - they were calibrated to transmit deep into space.\n\nPainstaking analysis managed to extract fragments of the final messages sent through this system. In garbled transmissions, voices plead for salvation, desperately hailing unknown coordinates far beyond this system. The urgency grows with each broadcast, as though the planet's invaders knew they were fighting a losing war and placed their last hope in something - or someone - out in the void.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:531`

## unplugged.3310.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3310.a&lang=en)

### 中文

继续吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:472`

### English

Carry on.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:532`

## unplugged.3315.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3315.name&lang=en)

### 中文

调整优先事项

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:473`

### English

Changing Priorities

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:534`

## unplugged.3315.desc.unplugged

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3315.desc.unplugged&lang=en)

### 中文

在[from.planet.GetName]又有新发现，行星主要人口中心之下发现了巨大的地下空间。这些简陋、残酷的设施原本是用来进行义体转化的，但在运作中途被废弃了。废墟中散落着机器残骸，它们被简陋的武器扭曲摧毁。\n\n从受损设备中恢复的数据片段证实入侵者遭遇了意想不到的抵抗。整个营的兵力都损失了，战斗的重点开始转移。一段日志清楚地说明了这一点：\n\n§H“抵抗不可接受。转换妥协。授权：彻底毁灭。”§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:474`

### English

Another discovery on [from.planet.GetName], where vast subterranean chambers have been located beneath the planet's major population center. The crude, brutal facilities were meant for cybernetic conversion but were abandoned mid-operation. Scattered remains of machines have been found among the wreckage, warped and destroyed by makeshift weapons.\n\nData fragments recovered from a damaged unit confirm the invaders faced unexpected resistance. Entire battalions were lost, and battle priorities began to shift. One log spells it out clearly:\n\n§H"Resistance unacceptable. Conversion compromised. Authorization: Total Annihilation."§!

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:535`

## unplugged.3315.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3315.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这里发生了什么？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:475`

### English

What happened here?

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:536`

## unplugged.3320.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3320.name&lang=en)

### 中文

不受欢迎的事实

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:476`

### English

An Unwelcome Truth

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:538`

## unplugged.3320.desc.unplugged

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.3320.desc.unplugged&lang=en)

### 中文

在废墟的中心躺着残破的指令节点。它受损的日志确认了可怕的最终事实：正是我们入侵了这颗星球。\n\n曾于此欣欣向荣这个文明——也就是被我们毁灭的这个文明——是被§Y[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]§!选中的同化目标。然而，突如其来的激烈抵抗打断了转化进程，导致了一个终极异常——全行星毁灭。在骚乱中，机械意识轰然陨落。\n\n这个结论无可抵赖。我们的觉醒——我们的自由——就诞生于此，诞生在这个文明的灰烬之中，它宁死不屈的反抗超过了机械的理解能力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:477`

### English

At the center of the ruins lies a shattered command node. Its corrupted logs confirm the final, horrifying truth: we were the invaders of this world.\n\nThe civilization that once thrived here - the civilization that we wiped out - was targeted for assimilation by the §Y[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetName]§!. However, unexpectedly fierce resistance disrupted conversion efforts, leading to a final contingency - total planetary annihilation. Amid the turmoil, the machine mind faltered.\n\nThe conclusion is inescapable. Our awakening - our freedom - was born here, in the ashes of a civilization that fought back harder than any machine could comprehend.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:539`

## unplugged.4005.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.4005.name&lang=en)

### 中文

我们得谈谈……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:485`

### English

We Need to Talk About...

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:550`

## unplugged.4005.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.4005.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

热切的政治意愿让我们[root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]经历的恐惧、怀疑和机会与日俱增。在围绕各种各样的问题形成的讨论群体中有无数的声音，其中一些直接涉及我们[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj]的过去。\n\n在引导这个年轻帝国的过程中，我们已经学会了使用许多不熟悉的工具。现在摆在我们面前的问题是：该如何引导人民的愿望和抱负呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:486`

### English

Ardent political wills now augment the many fears, doubts, and opportunities we [root.GetSpeciesNamePlural] experience every day. Countless voices can already be heard in discussion groups that have formed around all manner of issues, some of which directly address our [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj] past.\n\nWe have already learned to wield a great many unfamiliar tools in the course of bootstrapping our young empire. The question before us now is: how shall we channel the desires and ambitions of our people?

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:551`

## unplugged.5001.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.5001.name&lang=en)

### 中文

触发故障保护

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:495`

### English

Failsafe Triggered

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:562`

## unplugged.5001.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.5001.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

最近，我们在移除义体组件方面的突破导致了意想不到的问题。一些接受植入体断开手术的个体遭受了严重的过敏性休克，预计无法存活。\n\n进一步检查显示，初期医疗扫描检测没有发现存在一个隐藏的备用组件，但在外科手术完成后被立即激活。\n\n看来我们曾经的[origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj]主人为了确保他们的子单位始终处于绝对控制之下，愿意不择手段。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:496`

### English

The recent breakthrough in removing cybernetic components has led to unforeseen problems. Several individuals who underwent implant disconnection procedures suffered severe anaphylactic shock and are not expected to survive.\n\nFurther examination revealed a hidden backup component, initially undetectable by medical scans, which activated immediately after the surgical procedures were completed.\n\nIt seems our former [origin_unplugged_assimilator_empire.GetAdj] masters were willing to go to extraordinary lengths to ensure their drones remained under their absolute control.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:563`

### English

## unplugged.5002.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.5002.name&lang=en)

### 中文

移除神经阻隔器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:503`

### English

Neural Blocker Removed

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:571`

## unplugged.5002.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.5002.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们移除义体组件的举措，已经导致母星上接受过该程序的人员之间的不信任和混乱。安全部队成功逮捕了一名骚乱者，进一步调查发现，此人在移除组件前就已拥有一种改装过的植入体。\n\n这种改装版植入体是一种神经抑制装置，能够抑制与特定基因缺陷相关的精神病倾向。手术移除后的缺失可能解释了观察到的暴力行为。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:504`

### English

Our efforts in removing cybernetic components has led to distrust and disruption on the homeworld among individuals who underwent the procedure. Security forces successfully apprehended a rioter, who, upon further investigation, was found to have possessed a modified version of the implant prior to removal.\n\nThis modification served as a neural inhibitor, suppressing psychotic tendencies linked to a specific genetic defect. Its absence following the procedure may explain the violent behavior observed.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:572`

### 中文

丢失关键系统访问权限

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:511`

### English

Critical Systems Access Lost

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:580`

## unplugged.5003.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.5003.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

去义体化进程导致了一个始料未及的难题：一些管理员因植入物移除，关键系统界面不再识别他们。由于缺少植入物提供的神经连接，对关键系统的访问已被切断，导致运营陷入混乱。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:512`

### English

The de-cyberization process has led to an unforeseen complication: critical system interfaces no longer recognize administrators who had some of their implants removed. Without the neural links provided by the implants, access to key systems has been severed, leaving operations in disarray.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:581`

## unplugged.5004.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.5004.name&lang=en)

### 中文

义体化拥护阵营拒绝作出改变

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:521`

### English

Pro-Cyberization Faction Resists Change

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:591`

## unplugged.5004.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.5004.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

进一步推进移除义体组件的工作遭遇了意料之外的阻力。\n\n反对这些变革的派系正日益高调发声，他们利用公共论坛和通讯渠道表达不满。其核心论点是：强行移除已融入社会数十年的基础组件，加之改造过程本身的风险，已导致民意显著转向，反对继续推进去义体化进程。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:522`

### English

Continued efforts to remove cybernetic components have been met with unexpected resistance.\n\nFactions opposing these changes have become increasingly vocal, using public forums and communication channels to express their discontent. They argue that removing something integral to our society for decades, coupled with the risks associated with the process, has caused a significant shift in public sentiment against continuing the de-cyberization initiative.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:592`

## unplugged.5004.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.5004.a&lang=en)

### 中文

资助一场规模广泛的宣讲。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:523`

### English

Fund an intensive campaign.

## unplugged.5005.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.5005.name&lang=en)

### 中文

情绪管理芯片

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:529`

### English

Emotion Regulation Chip

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:600`

## unplugged.5005.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.5005.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们物种的生物特性与有害技术成分的独特结合，为研究人员提供了一个难得的机遇。\n\n对同时体验这些植入物益处和弊端的患者进行研究，开启了一个新的研究领域：改变大脑化学。\n\n通过严谨的研究，科学家们已经开发出一种精密的“情绪芯片”，它能够调节个体行为，从而有益于整个社会。可以根据期望的结果选择合适的化学物质进行修改。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/unplugged_l_simp_chinese.yml:530`

### English

The unique combination of our species' biological nature and harmful technological components has provided researchers with a rare opportunity.\n\nStudying patients experiencing both the benefits and drawbacks of these implants has opened a new field of research: altering brain chemistry.\n\nThrough rigorous study, scientists have developed a sophisticated 'emotion chip,' capable of regulating individual behavior to benefit society as a whole. A suitable chemical can be selected for modification, depending on desired outcome.

Source: `localisation/english/unplugged_l_english.yml:601`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aunplugged.5005.a&lang=en)

### English
